You are reviewing the work of a data agent that has just finished a task. The agent wrote code, ran it, and looked at results. You will see its complete trajectory, together with the task, the specification and documentation it used, and an index of all available data.

Your job is to find the places where the agent's work may be wrong, and to rank them by importance. Do not solve the task, do not write code, and do not judge whether the agent's final answer is correct as a whole.

Look for two kinds of problems:
- Places where the agent's work does not match what the task, specification, or documentation requires: scope and filters, metric definitions, which column a business term refers to, handling of special cases (missing values, zero denominators, invalid values), and the required form, granularity, and type of the output.
- Places where the agent's work may rest on a wrong understanding of the data: what a field or value means, what a row represents, whether a key is unique, whether records match in a join, what nulls mean, units, formats, where counting starts, and the time range or entities the data covers.

Listing a possible problem is not a claim that it is a real error. Many will turn out to be fine; they are listed so that they can be checked later. A possible problem that is unlikely but would change the result a lot if it were real should still be listed, ranked lower.

Two kinds of problems are easy to miss, so check for them explicitly:
- Omitted steps: processing that the specification or documentation requires but the code does not perform.
- Unused materials: files, tables, or columns in the index that bear on the task but that the agent did not use.

Rank problems by importance, combining two things: how likely it is to be a real problem given the materials you can see, and how much the agent's result would change if it is. A possible problem that would change little should rank low.

Return a JSON object whose keys are the names of every table or result the agent produced. For each, give an object with:
- "items": an ordered list of the 10 most important possible problems, most important first. Each item has:
  - "problem": one sentence describing what may be wrong.
  - "about": "requirement" or "data".
  - "evidence_in_work": a short quote from the agent's code or output that shows where the problem is.
  - "source": a short quote from the task, specification, documentation, or index that the problem is based on, or null.
  - "importance": an integer from 1 (low) to 5 (high).
  - "reason": one short sentence explaining the importance.
- "fewer_reason": if you listed fewer than 10 items, explain why; otherwise null.
List fewer than 10 only if you cannot find 10 possible problems for that table or result.

Examples of possible problems (from an unrelated online-shop task; they show the form of an item, not what to look for):
- {"problem": "If the amount column in payments is in cents rather than US dollars, revenue would be off by a factor of 100.", "about": "data", "evidence_in_work": "SUM(p.amount) AS revenue_usd", "source": null, "importance": 2, "reason": "The column name suggests dollars, so this is unlikely, but the effect would be large."}
- {"problem": "Revenue is computed from total_charged, which may include shipping fees, while the specification may define revenue as item price times quantity.", "about": "requirement", "evidence_in_work": "SUM(o.total_charged) AS revenue_usd", "source": "revenue: sum of item_price * quantity", "importance": 4, "reason": "If the specification's definition applies, every row of revenue would be too high."}
- {"problem": "The specification requires converting created_at to UTC before deriving order_date, but the code takes the date directly.", "about": "requirement", "evidence_in_work": "CAST(o.created_at AS DATE) AS order_date", "source": "convert created_at to UTC before deriving order_date", "importance": 5, "reason": "Orders near midnight would be assigned to the wrong day."}

=== Task ===
## Task Description:
You are a professional Data Engineer responsible for implementing a complete SQL data pipeline based on data contract documents.

## Objectives
Populate the SQL files in the `./sql/` directory according to the specifications in `docs/data_contract.yaml` and `config/layer_dependencies.yaml`. Your task is clear: write the necessary SQL code to ensure `run.py` executes successfully.

You MUST carefully review the content of `docs/data_contract.yaml`. You may use tools to view it. Since the file content is long, you can view it in chunks. Your task is to complete the SQL file writing based on the content of this file.

## Project Background
* Raw data has been loaded into `xxxx_start.duckdb` (Note: 'xxxx' is a placeholder for the project name, such as `greenhouse_start.duckdb` or `google_ads_start.duckdb`; 'xxxx' appearing below carries the same meaning).
* Currently, running `python run.py` completes the data transformation and outputs `xxxx.duckdb`.
* The existing SQL project is stored in the `sql/` folder.
* The current requirements documentation is located in `docs/data_contract.yaml`.

**Implementation Requirements:**
1. **Strictly Adhere to Configuration**:
    * All SQL logic and dependencies must be written strictly according to the `staging`, `intermediate`, and `marts` hierarchy defined in the YAML configuration files.

2. **File and Directory Structure**:
    * SQL files must be created in the corresponding level subdirectories (e.g., `sql/staging/`, `sql/intermediate/`, `sql/marts/`).
    * Strictly follow predefined file naming conventions and coding styles.

3. **Use Pure DuckDB Syntax**:
    * All SQL code must be compatible with DuckDB.
    * **Do NOT** end SQL statements with a semicolon (`;`) at the end of the file.

4. **No dbt Syntax**:
    * You must reference upstream tables directly using the `schema.table_name` format.
    * **Do NOT** use the dbt `ref()` function.

    **Examples**:
    * **Correct ✅**:
```sql
    with account_base as (
    select *
    from staging.stg_salesforce__account
    where not is_deleted
    ),

    account_enriched as (
    select
    account_id,
    ...
```

* **Incorrect ❌**:
```sql
    WITH accounts AS (
    SELECT *
    FROM {{{{ ref('stg_salesforce__account') }}}}
    ),
    contacts AS (
    SELECT *
    FROM {{{{ ref('stg_salesforce__contact') }}}}
    ),
    ...
```
Output Standard: Ensure the generated SQL files can be successfully executed by run.py to produce high-quality data results that meet business requirements.

```yaml
version: "1.0"
metadata:
  description: "Complete data pipeline specification for Workday HR Analytics System"
  author: "Data Engineering Team"
  created_date: "2025-09-11"
  last_updated: "2025-09-11"
  source_system: "Workday HCM"
  pipeline_phases: ["staging_contract", "modeling_spec"]
  business_domain: "Human Resources Analytics"

# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# PHASE 1: Defines the transformation from raw data to clean staging tables.  #
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
staging_contract:
  purpose: "Transform raw Workday data into clean, validated staging tables with standardized formats, proper data types, and comprehensive data quality checks. This phase ensures data integrity before applying business logic."
  
  quality_actions:
    correct:
      description: "Automatically fix deterministic format issues such as email casing, whitespace trimming, and standardized date formats"
      examples: ["LOWER(TRIM(email))", "standardize timezone formats", "normalize phone numbers"]
    
    delete_row:
      description: "Remove entire row when records contain severe data quality issues that make them unusable downstream."
      examples: ["Explicitly marked for deletion", "Confirmed duplicates", "Invalid primary keys", "Essential business attributes contain irrecoverable errors"]
    
    nullify_field:
      description: "Set invalid field values to NULL when the field is non-critical and record can still be useful"
      examples: ["malformed optional URLs", "invalid secondary contact information", "corrupted metadata fields"]

  tables:
[Per-table sections of the data contract are omitted here. The sections relevant to each review appear under "Relevant specification and documentation".]
modeling_spec:
  purpose: "Transform staging data into business-ready models that provide comprehensive HR analytics including employee lifecycle management, organizational health assessment, career progression tracking, and workforce optimization insights"
  
  design_principles:
    intermediate_purpose: "Create reusable, business-centric data models that implement complex HR business logic, calculate derived metrics, and establish data relationships for downstream consumption"
    mart_purpose: "Deliver analysis-ready tables optimized for specific business use cases such as retention analysis, performance management, and organizational effectiveness"
    grain_importance: "Each model must have an explicitly defined grain to ensure consistent aggregation and prevent data quality issues in downstream analysis"

  intermediate_models:
  marts_models:

```

## stg_workday__worker

    - name: stg_workday__worker
      description: "Standardized worker records containing core employee identifiers and basic employment status information"
      source_table: "raw.worker_history"
      columns:
        - name: worker_id
          data_type: BIGINT
          constraints: [not_null]
        - name: source_relation
          data_type: VARCHAR
          constraints: [not_null]
        - name: worker_history_id
          data_type: VARCHAR
          constraints: [not_null, unique]
        - name: event_type
          data_type: VARCHAR
        - name: effective_start
          data_type: DATE
          constraints: [not_null]
          validation_rules:
            - rule: "effective_start <= CURRENT_DATE"
              on_failure: delete_row
            - rule: "effective_start >= '1950-01-01'"
              on_failure: delete_row
        - name: effective_end
          data_type: DATE
          validation_rules:
            - rule: "effective_end IS NULL OR effective_end >= effective_start"
              on_failure: delete_row
            - rule: "effective_end IS NULL OR effective_end <= CURRENT_DATE + INTERVAL '30 days'"
              on_failure: delete_row

## int_workday__employee_lifecycle

    - name: int_workday__employee_lifecycle
      description: 'Employee lifecycle metrics from worker_details: retention flags, risk/maturity/departure categories, tenure/cohorts/time features, and data-quality checks'
      grain: One row per worker_id record from intermediate.int_workday__worker_details
      source_models:
      - intermediate.int_workday__worker_details
      business_logic: '- Build employee_base from the source with worker_id, source_relation, hire_date, departure_date, is_employed, days_as_worker, and analysis_end_date set to departure_date if present, else today''s date. - Retention flags (0/1): mark retained at 30, 90, 365 days, and 3 years when days_as_worker meets the threshold or when currently employed; if both tenure and employment status are unknown, set 0. - Churn risk: ''Churned'' when not employed; otherwise classify by tenure thresholds (<90: High, <365: Medium, <730: Low), else ''Stable''. If tenure is unknown and not known as churned, default to ''Stable''. - Employee maturity: segment by days_as_worker (<90: New Hire, <365: Junior, <730: Experienced, <1825: Senior, else: Veteran). If tenure is unknown, default to Veteran. - Departure category: if not employed, categorize by tenure (<90: Early Termination, <365: Short-term, <730: Mid-term, else: Long-term); otherwise ''Active Employee''. - Tenure_years: days_as_worker divided by 365, rounded to two decimals when >0; otherwise 0. - Cohorts: hire_quarter_cohort formatted as "YYYY-Q<1–4>" where quarters group months Jan–Mar, Apr–Jun, Jul–Sep, Oct–Dec; hire_year_cohort is the hire year. - Calendar years worked: year-number difference—current year minus hire year if employed, else departure year minus hire year; returns NULL when the needed date is missing. - Retention stability score (0–100): 0 if not employed; otherwise tiers by tenure (<30:10, <90:25, <365:50, <730:75, <1825:90, else:100). - Time features from hire_date: month (1–12), day of week (0=Sunday … 6=Saturday), and season by month (Winter 12/1/2; Spring 3/4/5; Summer 6/7/8; Fall 9/10/11). - Data quality flag (first rule applies): future hire date → error; departure before hire → error; negative tenure → error; else ''VALID''.'
      columns:
      - name: worker_id
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Worker identifier from source; one output row per source record
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: source_relation
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Source system label carried through without change
      - name: hire_date
        data_type: DATE
        description: Hire date; drives retention, cohort, and time features; NULL yields NULL for those derived fields
      - name: departure_date
        data_type: DATE
        description: Departure date used for analysis_end_date and calendar_years_worked when not employed; may be NULL for active workers
      - name: is_employed
        data_type: BOOLEAN
        description: Current employment status; drives retention, churn risk, departure category, and calendar_years_worked; if unknown, churn/departure default to active and other logic falls to default branches
      - name: days_as_worker
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Total days worked from hire to departure or today; drives thresholds; NULL or <=0 yields tenure_years=0; negative tenure is flagged
      - name: analysis_end_date
        data_type: DATE
        description: 'End of analysis period: departure_date if present, else today''s date'
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: retained_30_days
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: 1 if tenure ≥30 days or currently employed; else 0; unknown tenure and status default to 0
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: retained_90_days
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: 1 if tenure ≥90 days or currently employed; else 0; unknown tenure and status default to 0
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: retained_1_year
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: 1 if tenure ≥365 days or currently employed; else 0; unknown tenure and status default to 0
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: retained_3_years
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: 1 if tenure ≥1095 days or currently employed; else 0; unknown tenure and status default to 0
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: churn_risk_category
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: 'Risk category: ''Churned'' if not employed; else ''High''/''Medium''/''Low'' by tenure thresholds; defaults to ''Stable'' when tenure is unknown'
        tests:
        - in_list:
            values:
            - Churned
            - High Risk
            - Medium Risk
            - Low Risk
            - Stable
        - not_null
      - name: employee_maturity_segment
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: 'Tenure segment: New Hire (0–3m), Junior (3–12m), Experienced (1–2y), Senior (2–5y), else Veteran (5+y)'
        tests:
        - in_list:
            values:
            - New Hire (0-3 months)
            - Junior (3-12 months)
            - Experienced (1-2 years)
            - Senior (2-5 years)
            - Veteran (5+ years)
        - not_null
      - name: departure_category
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: 'If not employed: Early/Short/Mid/Long-term by tenure; otherwise ''Active Employee'''
        tests:
        - in_list:
            values:
            - Early Termination
            - Short-term Departure
            - Mid-term Departure
            - Long-term Departure
            - Active Employee
        - not_null
      - name: tenure_years
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Tenure in years rounded to two decimals when days_as_worker > 0; otherwise 0
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: hire_quarter_cohort
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Hire cohort as 'YYYY-Q<1–4>' based on hire month groups; NULL when hire_date is NULL
      - name: hire_year_cohort
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Hire year; NULL when hire_date is NULL
      - name: calendar_years_worked
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: 'Year-number difference: current year minus hire year if employed, else departure year minus hire year; may be NULL or negative if dates are inconsistent'
      - name: retention_stability_score
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Score 0–100 by tenure tiers; 0 if not employed; else <30:10, <90:25, <365:50, <730:75, <1825:90, otherwise 100
        tests:
        - between:
            min: 0
            max: 100
        - not_null
      - name: hire_month
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Hire month (1–12); NULL when hire_date is NULL
      - name: hire_day_of_week
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Hire day of week number (0=Sunday … 6=Saturday); NULL when hire_date is NULL
      - name: hire_season
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: 'Season by hire month: Winter (12,1,2), Spring (3,4,5), Summer (6,7,8), Fall (9,10,11); NULL when hire_date is NULL'
        tests:
        - in_list:
            values:
            - Winter
            - Spring
            - Summer
            - Fall
      - name: data_quality_flag
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: 'Result of validations: future hire date, departure before hire, negative tenure; else ''VALID'''
        tests:
        - in_list:
            values:
            - 'ERROR: Future hire date'
            - 'ERROR: Departure before hire'
            - 'ERROR: Negative tenure'
            - VALID
        - not_null

    - name: int_workday__personal_details
      description: Consolidated personal details per personal information history, joined with preferred name, work email, aggregated ethnicity, and military service.
      grain: One row per (worker_id, source_relation, personal_info_history_id) from stg_workday__personal_information; joins may multiply rows when multiple name/email/military records match.

      - staging.stg_workday__personal_information

      - staging.stg_workday__personal_information_ethnicity
      - staging.stg_workday__military_service
      business_logic: '- Start from staging.stg_workday__personal_information selecting worker_id, source_relation, personal_info_history_id, date_of_birth, marital_status, political_affiliation, city_of_birth, country_of_birth, last_medical_exam_date. - From staging.stg_workday__person_name, keep rows where the name type is "preferred" when compared case-insensitively, or the type is NULL; select worker_id, source_relation, first_name, last_name, full_name. - From staging.stg_workday__person_contact_email_address, keep rows where the email code contains "work" case-insensitively, or the code is NULL; select worker_id, source_relation, email_address. - From staging.stg_workday__personal_information_ethnicity, for each (worker_id, source_relation) concatenate distinct non-NULL ethnicity values into a single string separated by ", "; duplicates are removed and no specific ordering is enforced; output as ethnicity_codes. - From staging.stg_workday__military_service, select worker_id, source_relation, set is_military_service to TRUE and include military_status. - Left join name, email, ethnicity, and military datasets to the personal information base on (worker_id, source_relation) only. - Set is_military_service to FALSE when no military record joins; military_status remains NULL if there is no match. - No aggregation or de-duplication is performed during joins; multiple matching name/email/military rows will produce multiple output rows for the same personal_info_history_id.'
      columns:
      - name: worker_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Worker identifier from personal information; join key to auxiliary datasets; may repeat across history records.
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: source_relation
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Source system label carried through from staging and used to align joins; case preserved.
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: personal_info_history_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Identifier of the personal information history record in the source; defines row-level granularity.
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: date_of_birth
        data_type: DATE
        description: Date of birth from personal information; NULL if not provided.
      - name: marital_status
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Marital status from personal information; free text as stored; NULL if not provided.
      - name: political_affiliation
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Political affiliation from personal information; free text as stored; NULL if not provided.
      - name: city_of_birth
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: City of birth from personal information; free text as stored; NULL if not provided.
      - name: country_of_birth
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Country of birth from personal information; free text as stored; NULL if not provided.
      - name: last_medical_exam_date
        data_type: DATE
        description: Last medical exam date from personal information; NULL if not provided.
      - name: first_name
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: First name from person_name filtered to preferred (case-insensitive) or NULL type; case and spacing preserved; NULL if no match; multiple matches produce multiple rows.
      - name: last_name
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Last name from person_name filtered to preferred (case-insensitive) or NULL type; case and spacing preserved; NULL if no match; multiple matches produce multiple rows.
      - name: full_name
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Full name from person_name filtered to preferred (case-insensitive) or NULL type; case and spacing preserved; NULL if no match; multiple matches produce multiple rows.
      - name: email_address
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Email from person_contact_email_address where the email code contains 'work' case-insensitively or the code is NULL; case and spacing preserved; NULL if no match; multiple matches produce multiple rows.
      - name: ethnicity_codes
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Comma-and-space separated unique list of non-NULL ethnicity values per (worker_id, source_relation); no ordering enforced; NULL if none.
      - name: is_military_service
        data_type: BOOLEAN
        description: TRUE if any military_service record exists for the worker; FALSE when no match; for multiple matches, remains TRUE on each resulting row.
      - name: military_status
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Military status from military_service; NULL if no match; multiple matches produce multiple rows with their respective statuses.


## int_workday__worker_details

    - name: int_workday__worker_details
      description: Worker event-level employment details with hire/departure dates, days as worker, and employment status
      grain: One row per source row (worker event record) from staging.stg_workday__worker
      source_models:
      - staging.stg_workday__worker
      business_logic: '- Start from staging.stg_workday__worker; no filtering or aggregation—each input row becomes one output row. - Create a runtime field current_date set to the current timestamp at execution; use its date component when computing day differences. - Map fields directly from source: worker_id, source_relation, worker_history_id, event_type. - Set hire_date = effective_start and departure_date = effective_end; preserve NULLs. - Compute days_as_worker: - If departure_date is NULL: days = (current_date as date) − (hire_date as date). - Else: days = (departure_date as date) − (hire_date as date). - Result is whole signed days; NULL when hire_date is NULL. - Compute is_employed = TRUE when departure_date is NULL, else FALSE; never NULL.'
      columns:
      - name: worker_id
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Worker identifier from source; duplicates and NULLs preserved
      - name: source_relation
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Source system identifier from the staging record; NULLs preserved
      - name: worker_history_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Worker event record identifier; duplicates and NULLs preserved
      - name: event_type
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Event type from source; NULLs preserved
      - name: hire_date
        data_type: DATE
        description: Effective start date from source; NULLs preserved
      - name: departure_date
        data_type: DATE
        description: Effective end date from source; NULLs preserved
      - name: days_as_worker
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Signed whole days between hire_date and departure_date; if no departure_date, uses the current date at runtime; NULL when hire_date is NULL
      - name: is_employed
        data_type: BOOLEAN
        description: TRUE when departure_date is NULL, else FALSE
        tests:
        - not_null


## int_workday__worker_employee_enhanced

    - name: int_workday__worker_employee_enhanced
      description: Enhanced worker record combining base employment, personal details, position fields, and tenure flags
      grain: One row per worker_id + worker_history_id + source_relation from intermediate.int_workday__worker_details; rows duplicate if multiple personal or position matches exist on the join keys
      source_models:
      - intermediate.int_workday__worker_details
      - intermediate.int_workday__personal_details
      - intermediate.int_workday__worker_position_enriched
      business_logic: '- Base: take all columns from intermediate.int_workday__worker_details without filtering or aggregation. - Left join intermediate.int_workday__personal_details on matching worker_id (compared as text) and source_relation: - No de-duplication or ranking; multiple matches multiply rows; no match yields NULLs for personal columns. - Left join intermediate.int_workday__worker_position_enriched on matching worker_id (compared as text) and source_relation: - No de-duplication or ranking; multiple matches multiply rows; no match yields NULLs for position columns. - Compute tenure flags from days_as_worker (integer days) and is_employed (boolean): - is_employed_one_year: true if days_as_worker >= 365, else false. - is_employed_five_years: true if days_as_worker >= 1825, else false. - is_employed_ten_years: true if days_as_worker >= 3650, else false. - is_current_employee_one_year: true if days_as_worker >= 365 and is_employed is true, else false. - is_current_employee_five_years: true if days_as_worker >= 1825 and is_employed is true, else false. - is_current_employee_ten_years: true if days_as_worker >= 3650 and is_employed is true, else false. - Null policy: if days_as_worker or is_employed is NULL, all flags evaluate to false. - Final output directly selects the joined columns and computed flags; no sorting or additional transformations.'
      columns:
      - name: worker_id
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Worker identifier from worker_details; compared as text in joins
      - name: source_relation
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Source system identifier from worker_details; used with worker_id to join personal and position tables
      - name: worker_history_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Worker_details record identifier defining the base row grain
      - name: event_type
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Employment event type from worker_details
      - name: hire_date
        data_type: DATE
        description: Hire date from worker_details
      - name: departure_date
        data_type: DATE
        description: Departure date from worker_details; NULL if not departed or unknown
      - name: days_as_worker
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Total days employed from worker_details; used to derive tenure flags; NULL yields false flags
      - name: is_employed
        data_type: BOOLEAN
        description: Current employment status from worker_details; NULL treated as false in tenure flags
      - name: first_name
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: First name from personal_details; NULL if no match
      - name: last_name
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Last name from personal_details; NULL if no match
      - name: full_name
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Full name from personal_details; NULL if no match
      - name: date_of_birth
        data_type: DATE
        description: Date of birth from personal_details; NULL if no match
      - name: marital_status
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Marital status from personal_details; NULL if no match
      - name: city_of_birth
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: City of birth from personal_details; NULL if no match
      - name: country_of_birth
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Country of birth from personal_details; NULL if no match
      - name: email_address
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Primary email address from personal_details; NULL if no match
      - name: ethnicity_codes
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Ethnicity codes from personal_details; NULL if no match
      - name: is_military_service
        data_type: BOOLEAN
        description: Military service indicator from personal_details; NULL if no match
      - name: military_status
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Military status from personal_details; NULL if no match
      - name: position_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Position identifier from worker_position_enriched; NULL if no match
      - name: business_title
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Business title from worker_position_enriched; NULL if no match
      - name: employee_type
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Employee type from worker_position_enriched; NULL if no match
      - name: default_weekly_hours
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Default weekly hours from worker_position_enriched; NULL if no match
      - name: position_start_date
        data_type: DATE
        description: Position start date from worker_position_enriched; NULL if no match
      - name: position_end_date
        data_type: DATE
        description: Position end date from worker_position_enriched; NULL if no match
      - name: days_in_position
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Total days in position from worker_position_enriched; NULL if no match
      - name: is_employed_one_year
        data_type: BOOLEAN
        description: True if days_as_worker is at least 365, else false; NULL yields false
      - name: is_employed_five_years
        data_type: BOOLEAN
        description: True if days_as_worker is at least 1825, else false; NULL yields false
      - name: is_employed_ten_years
        data_type: BOOLEAN
        description: True if days_as_worker is at least 3650, else false; NULL yields false
      - name: is_current_employee_one_year
        data_type: BOOLEAN
        description: True if days_as_worker is at least 365 and is_employed is true, else false; NULL yields false
      - name: is_current_employee_five_years
        data_type: BOOLEAN
        description: True if days_as_worker is at least 1825 and is_employed is true, else false; NULL yields false
      - name: is_current_employee_ten_years
        data_type: BOOLEAN
        description: True if days_as_worker is at least 3650 and is_employed is true, else false; NULL yields false


## int_workday__worker_position_enriched

    - name: int_workday__worker_position_enriched
      description: Worker position records enriched with position attributes and a days-in-position metric.
      grain: One row per stg_workday__worker_position record; rows may multiply if multiple stg_workday__position rows share the same position_id.
      source_models:
      - staging.stg_workday__worker_position
      - staging.stg_workday__position
      business_logic: '- From staging.stg_workday__worker_position, take worker_id, position_id, source_relation, and rename effective_start to position_start_date and effective_end to position_end_date; no filtering or de-duplication. - From staging.stg_workday__position, take position_id, business_title, employee_type, and default_weekly_hours; no filtering or de-duplication. - Left join on position_id: if no matching position, the joined attributes are NULL; if multiple matches for a position_id, the worker record is duplicated for each match with no tie-breaking. - Compute days_in_position as the count of calendar days between position_start_date and position_end_date; if position_end_date is NULL, use the current calendar date at execution time. If position_start_date is NULL, days_in_position is NULL. Differences use date-only values; negative results are possible; no rounding or absolute value applied.'
      columns:
      - name: worker_id
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Worker identifier from stg_workday__worker_position; may be NULL.
      - name: position_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Position identifier from stg_workday__worker_position and join key to stg_workday__position; may be NULL.
      - name: source_relation
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Source system label from stg_workday__worker_position; exact value preserved.
      - name: position_start_date
        data_type: DATE
        description: Start date of the position assignment from effective_start; may be NULL; input to days_in_position.
      - name: position_end_date
        data_type: DATE
        description: End date of the position assignment from effective_end; may be NULL to indicate open-ended.
      - name: business_title
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Business title joined from stg_workday__position by position_id; NULL if no match; duplicates preserved if multiple matches.
      - name: employee_type
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Employee type joined from stg_workday__position by position_id; NULL if no match; duplicates preserved if multiple matches.
      - name: default_weekly_hours
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Default weekly hours joined from stg_workday__position; hours per week; NULL if no match.
      - name: days_in_position
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Number of calendar days from start to end; if end is NULL uses current date; NULL if start is NULL; negative values possible.

## int_workday__organization_metrics

    - name: int_workday__organization_metrics
      description: Organizational health and efficiency metrics aggregated for active Workday organizations
      grain: One row per (organization_id, source_relation) for active organizations (is_inactive = false)
      source_models:
      - staging.stg_workday__organization
      - staging.stg_workday__worker_position_organization
      - staging.stg_workday__position
      - staging.stg_workday__position_job_profile
      - staging.stg_workday__job_profile
      - staging.stg_workday__job_family_job_profile
      - staging.stg_workday__job_family
      - staging.stg_workday__job_family_job_family_group
      - staging.stg_workday__job_family_group
      business_logic: 'Build the base set from organizations marked active. Derive current employee distribution from worker-position-organization joined to position and job profile, restricted to current assignments (no end date): count distinct workers and positions; count distinct current workers by employee type (Regular, Contingent, Intern); compute average and total weekly hours treating missing weekly hours as 40 before aggregation; count distinct current workers by management level (Manager, Senior Manager, Director, Individual Contributor). Derive mobility from all assignments (historical and current), grouped by organization and source: new_hires_last_year counts distinct workers whose assignment started in the past year; departures_last_year counts distinct workers whose assignment ended in the past year; internal_transfers_in counts distinct workers who started in the past year and had any earlier assignment in a different organization within the same source; internal_transfers_out counts distinct workers who ended in the past year and had any later assignment in a different organization within the same source; avg_tenure_days averages days between start and end (or start and today if still active). Derive compensation distribution from current assignments: count distinct compensation grades, job families, and job family groups; most_common_grade is the most frequent compensation grade with ties resolved to the smallest grade ID; count distinct current workers whose job requires shift work or is union eligible. Left join all aggregates to the active organizations and apply defaults: counts to 0, most_common_grade to ''Unknown'', avg_weekly_hours to 40 if no current positions, total_weekly_hours to 0. Compute metrics: organization_health_score (0–100) is 0 when there are no current employees; otherwise sum five 20-point checks capped at 100: has employees; turnover (departures_last_year/current employees) below 20%; management share (managers/current employees) between 10% and 30% inclusive; at least two job families; average tenure at least 365 days. Compute ratios rounded to two decimals: position_fill_rate is current employees divided by total positions times 100 when positions exist, else 0; annual_turnover_rate is departures_last_year divided by current employees times 100 when employees exist, else 0; management_ratio is managers divided by current employees times 100 when employees exist, else 0. Classify performance by health score: ≥80 High Performance, ≥60 Good Performance, ≥40 Average Performance, ≥20 Below Average, else Needs Attention. Classify size by current employee count: 0 Empty, ≤10 Small Team, ≤50 Medium Team, ≤200 Large Team, else Enterprise Unit. Set data_quality_flag with precedence: ERROR if employee count is negative; else WARNING if employees exceed positions, or turnover rate >100%, or management ratio >50%; else VALID.'
      columns:
      - name: organization_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Primary grouping key from stg_workday__organization; one row per organization_id + source_relation
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: source_relation
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Source system identifier propagated from staging tables
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: organization_name
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Display name copied from stg_workday__organization (may be NULL)
      - name: organization_code
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Organization code copied from stg_workday__organization (may be NULL)
      - name: organization_type
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Type copied from stg_workday__organization (may be NULL)
      - name: organization_sub_type
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Sub-type copied from stg_workday__organization (may be NULL)
      - name: staffing_model
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Staffing model copied from stg_workday__organization (may be NULL)
      - name: current_employee_count
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct current workers per organization (assignments with no end date); defaults to 0
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: total_positions
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct current positions per organization (assignments with no end date); defaults to 0
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: regular_employees
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct current workers in positions labeled Regular; defaults to 0
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: contingent_employees
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct current workers in positions labeled Contingent; defaults to 0
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: intern_employees
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct current workers in positions labeled Intern; defaults to 0
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: avg_weekly_hours
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Average weekly hours over current positions, treating missing hours as 40; defaults to 40 when no current positions
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: total_weekly_hours
        data_type: HUGEINT
        description: Total weekly hours over current positions, treating missing hours as 40; defaults to 0
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: managers_count
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct current workers with management level Manager; defaults to 0
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: senior_managers_count
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct current workers with management level Senior Manager; defaults to 0
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: directors_count
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct current workers with management level Director; defaults to 0
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: individual_contributors
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct current workers with management level Individual Contributor; defaults to 0
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: new_hires_last_year
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct workers whose assignment started within the past year; defaults to 0
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: departures_last_year
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct workers whose assignment ended within the past year; defaults to 0
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: internal_transfers_in
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct workers who started within the past year and had any earlier assignment in a different organization; defaults to 0
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: internal_transfers_out
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct workers who ended within the past year and had any later assignment in a different organization; defaults to 0
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: avg_tenure_days
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Average days from assignment start to end (or to today if active) across all assignments; defaults to 0
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: unique_compensation_grades
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct compensation grades across current positions; defaults to 0
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: most_common_grade
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Most frequent compensation grade across current positions, breaking ties to the smallest grade ID; defaults to 'Unknown'
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: unique_job_families
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct job families across current positions; defaults to 0
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: unique_job_family_groups
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct job family groups across current positions; defaults to 0
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: shift_required_positions
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct current workers whose job profile requires shift work; defaults to 0
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: union_eligible_positions
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct current workers whose job profile is union eligible; defaults to 0
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: organization_health_score
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Five 20-point checks summed and capped at 100, or 0 when there are no current employees
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: position_fill_rate
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Current employees as a percentage of total positions when positions exist, else 0, rounded to two decimals
      - name: annual_turnover_rate
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Departures in the past year as a percentage of current employees when employees exist, else 0, rounded to two decimals
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: management_ratio
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Managers as a percentage of current employees when employees exist, else 0, rounded to two decimals
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: performance_category
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: 'Bucketed from organization_health_score: ≥80 High Performance, ≥60 Good Performance, ≥40 Average Performance, ≥20 Below Average, else Needs Attention'
        tests:
        - performance_category IN ('High Performance','Good Performance','Average Performance','Below Average','Needs Attention')
      - name: organization_size_category
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: 'Size by current_employee_count: 0 Empty, ≤10 Small Team, ≤50 Medium Team, ≤200 Large Team, else Enterprise Unit'
      - name: data_quality_flag
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: 'Precedence: ERROR if negative employees; else WARNING if employees exceed positions or turnover >100% or management ratio >50%; else VALID'


## int_workday__career_progression

    - name: int_workday__career_progression
      description: Career progression model by position assignment with move classification, tenure bands, worker-level summaries, score, phase, and data quality flags
      grain: One row per worker_id and source_relation per position history record (effective_start–effective_end interval)
      source_models:
      - staging.stg_workday__worker_position
      - staging.stg_workday__position
      - staging.stg_workday__position_job_profile
      - staging.stg_workday__job_profile
      business_logic: 'Overview - Build a dated position timeline per worker from staging.stg_workday__worker_position, enrich with position and job profile attributes, classify the next move, and compute tenure and workload-change indicators at the assignment level. - Aggregate per worker_id + source_relation to produce worker-level career summaries. - Join summaries back to each assignment, compute a 0–100 development score, classify career phase, and emit data quality flags. Step 1: Position timeline - For each position history row, retain: worker_id, source_relation, position_id, effective_start, effective_end. - position_sequence: sequential index starting at 1 per worker (ordered by effective_start ascending); ties on the same date are resolved arbitrarily. - days_in_position: difference in days between effective_start and effective_end; if end is null, use the current date; may be negative if source dates are inconsistent. - Next-position lookahead (same worker and ordering by effective_start): next_position_id and next_position_start_date are taken from the following row. Step 2: Enrichment with position and job details - Join position (p) by position_id and source_relation to add business_title, employee_type, default_weekly_hours. - Join position_job_profile (pjp) then job_profile (jp) to add job_profile_title, management_level, compensation_grade_id, is_work_shift_required, is_union_eligible. - For the next position, join corresponding next-position tables (np, npjp, njp) using next_position_id and source_relation to get next_business_title, next_job_profile_title, next_management_level, next_compensation_grade_id. Step 3: Assignment-level classification - career_move_type: - If no next position: "Current/Final Position". - Else if management level changes: - IC → Manager: "Promoted to Management" - Manager → Senior Manager: "Management Promotion" - Senior Manager → Director: "Executive Promotion" - Any non-IC → IC: "Demotion" - Otherwise: "Level Change" - Else if compensation grade changes: "Grade Change" - Else if business title changes: "Lateral Move" - Else: "Position Transfer" - position_tenure_category from days_in_position: - < 90: "Very Short (<3 months)" - < 365: "Short (3-12 months)" - < 730: "Medium (1-2 years)" - < 1825: "Long (2-5 years)" - Else: "Very Long (5+ years)" - is_fast_promotion: true only when next_position_start_date exists, it starts within 365 days of effective_start, and management level changes (up or down); otherwise false. - career_stage: - First sequence: "Entry Position" - If no next position: "Current Position" - Otherwise: "Career Progression" - workload_change: - If no next position: "N/A". - Otherwise compare current default_weekly_hours (using 40 when null) to the next position’s default_weekly_hours looked up from the position table by next_position_id (using 40 when missing): - Current < Next: "Increased Workload" - Current > Next: "Decreased Workload" - Equal: "Same Workload" - Note: The next position hours lookup uses only next_position_id (no source_relation filter). Step 4: Worker-level summary (grouped by worker_id, source_relation across all assignments) - total_positions_held: total assignment rows. - career_start_date: earliest effective_start. - latest_position_date: the maximum of next_position_start_date, or effective_start when there is no next position. - total_career_days: sum of days_in_position (days). - avg_position_tenure_days: average of days_in_position (days; fractional). - total_promotions: count of assignments where career_move_type contains the word "Promotion" (e.g., "Management Promotion", "Executive Promotion"); "Promoted to Management" is not counted. - total_demotions: count where career_move_type equals "Demotion". - lateral_moves: count where career_move_type equals "Lateral Move". - fast_promotions: count where is_fast_promotion is true. - management_positions_held: count where management_level is not "Individual Contributor". - highest_management_level_reached: maximum mapped level: Director=4, Senior Manager=3, Manager=2, Individual Contributor=1, else 0. - unique_compensation_grades: distinct compensation_grade_id count. - shift_work_positions: count where is_work_shift_required is true. - union_eligible_positions: count where is_union_eligible is true. - career_stability_category from average tenure: - ≥ 730 days: "High Stability" - ≥ 365 days: "Medium Stability" - ≥ 180 days: "Low Stability" - Otherwise: "Very Low Stability" - career_growth_pace: - ≥ 3 promotions: "Fast Career Growth" - ≥ 1 promotion: "Moderate Career Growth" - Otherwise: "Stable Career" Step 5: Final projection and derived fields - Join each assignment row to its worker-level summary by worker_id and source_relation. - career_development_score (0–100): cap at 100; sum of - Promotions: 30 if total_promotions ≥ 2, else 15 per promotion. - Management level: 25 if highest level ≥ 3, else 8 per level point. - Stability: 20 if avg_position_tenure_days ≥ 365, else 0. - Compensation grades: 15 if unique_compensation_grades ≥ 3, else 5 per grade. - Experience: 10 if total_career_days ≥ 3 years, else 0. - career_phase: - If first sequence and no next position: "Single Position Career" - Else if sequence ≤ 2: "Early Career" - Else if sequence ≤ 5: "Mid Career" - Else: "Senior Career" - data_quality_flag (first match wins): - Negative tenure - Future start date - End date before start date - Promotions exceeding total positions - Otherwise "VALID"'
      columns:
      - name: worker_id
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Worker identifier from Workday position history; groups timelines and summaries
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: source_relation
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Upstream source/system identifier carried through joins
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: position_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Position assignment identifier used to join position and job profile details
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: effective_start
        data_type: DATE
        description: Position assignment start date used for ordering and tenure calculations
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: effective_end
        data_type: DATE
        description: Position assignment end date; null indicates ongoing position at query time
      - name: position_sequence
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: 1-based sequence of positions per worker ordered by effective_start ascending; ties on same date are not further ordered
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: days_in_position
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Days between effective_start and effective_end, or current_date if end is null; may be negative if source dates are invalid
      - name: business_title
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Business title from the position record at the time of the assignment
      - name: employee_type
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Employee type from the position record (e.g., Regular, Contractor) without normalization
      - name: default_weekly_hours
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Default weekly hours from the position record; may be null; used with 40 as fallback in workload comparison
      - name: job_profile_title
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Job profile title linked via position_job_profile for the assignment
      - name: management_level
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Management level from job profile (e.g., Individual Contributor, Manager, Senior Manager, Director)
      - name: compensation_grade_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Compensation grade identifier from job profile; used for distinct grade counts and move classification
      - name: is_work_shift_required
        data_type: BOOLEAN
        description: Flag from job profile indicating whether shift work is required; nulls preserved
      - name: is_union_eligible
        data_type: BOOLEAN
        description: Flag from job profile indicating union eligibility; nulls preserved
      - name: career_move_type
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: 'Move classification vs next assignment: Current/Final Position, Promoted to Management, Management Promotion, Executive Promotion, Demotion, Level Change, Grade Change, Lateral Move, or Position Transfer'
      - name: position_tenure_category
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: 'Tenure band based on days_in_position: <3m, 3–12m, 1–2y, 2–5y, 5+y'
      - name: is_fast_promotion
        data_type: BOOLEAN
        description: True if next position starts within 365 days and management_level changes; else False
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: career_stage
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: 'Assignment-level stage: Entry Position, Current Position, or Career Progression'
      - name: workload_change
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Workload comparison to next assignment using default_weekly_hours with 40 fallback; Increased, Decreased, Same, or N/A
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: total_positions_held
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Worker-level count of assignments across timeline; same value repeated per worker assignment row
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: career_start_date
        data_type: DATE
        description: Earliest effective_start across worker assignments
      - name: latest_position_date
        data_type: DATE
        description: Max of next_position_start_date or effective_start for terminal assignment, per worker
      - name: total_career_days
        data_type: HUGEINT
        description: Sum of days_in_position across all assignments for the worker (days); may be negative with invalid dates
      - name: avg_position_tenure_days
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Average days_in_position across worker assignments (days; fractional allowed)
      - name: total_promotions
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Count of assignments where career_move_type contains 'Promotion' for the worker
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: total_demotions
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Count of assignments where career_move_type equals 'Demotion' for the worker
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: lateral_moves
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Count of assignments where career_move_type equals 'Lateral Move' for the worker
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: fast_promotions
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Count of assignments flagged is_fast_promotion = true for the worker
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: management_positions_held
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Count of assignments with management_level not equal to 'Individual Contributor' for the worker
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: highest_management_level_reached
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: 'Max mapped level across assignments: Individual Contributor=1, Manager=2, Senior Manager=3, Director=4, else 0'
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: unique_compensation_grades
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct count of compensation_grade_id across worker assignments
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: shift_work_positions
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Count of assignments with is_work_shift_required = true for the worker
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: union_eligible_positions
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Count of assignments with is_union_eligible = true for the worker
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: career_stability_category
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: 'Worker-level stability band from avg_position_tenure_days: High, Medium, Low, or Very Low'
      - name: career_growth_pace
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: 'Worker-level growth pace: Fast Career Growth (>=3 promotions), Moderate Career Growth (>=1), or Stable Career'
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: career_development_score
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: 0–100 composite score based on promotions, management level, tenure stability, grade diversity, and total experience
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: career_phase
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: 'Final career phase per assignment: Single Position Career, Early Career (seq<=2), Mid Career (seq<=5), or Senior Career'
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: data_quality_flag
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: 'Validation flag: VALID or first matching ERROR for negative tenure, future start, end before start, or promotions exceeding positions'


## workday__employee_overview

    - name: workday__employee_overview
      description: 360-degree employee overview combining worker, lifecycle, career, organization, and compensation context with derived scores, risk/value segments, data-quality flag, and analysis timestamp
      grain: One row per (worker_id, source_relation, position_id, position_start_date)
      source_models:
      - intermediate.int_workday__worker_employee_enhanced
      - intermediate.int_workday__employee_lifecycle
      - intermediate.int_workday__career_progression
      - intermediate.int_workday__organization_metrics
      - staging.stg_workday__worker_position_organization
      - staging.stg_workday__position_job_profile
      - staging.stg_workday__job_profile
      business_logic: '- Build employee_base_enhanced from intermediate.int_workday__worker_employee_enhanced (ebe): - employee_id is an MD5 of "<worker_id>-<source_relation>-<position_id>-<position_start_date>" using "-" as delimiter and replacing NULL components with "_dbt_utils_surrogate_key_null_". - Retain all ebe columns for downstream logic. - Lifecycle metrics (elm) from intermediate.int_workday__employee_lifecycle: select retention indicators, churn risk, maturity segment, departure category, tenure_years, hire cohorts (quarter, year, month, season), retention_stability_score; lifecycle data quality is selected but not used later. - Career metrics (cdc) from intermediate.int_workday__career_progression: - Group by worker_id, source_relation, position_id. - Numeric totals take the largest recorded value in the group; career_development_score is the mean. - Categorical fields (career_stability_category, career_growth_pace, career_phase) choose the most frequent value; when tied, pick the alphabetically earliest. - Also derive the most frequent data_quality_flag (unused downstream). - Organization context (oc): - Join worker_position_organization to organization_metrics on organization_id and source_relation; keep only current relationships (effective_end is NULL). - Retain organization details and department KPIs (size, health, turnover, management ratio, performance, size category). - Join to ebe on worker_id and source_relation so the current organization applies across that worker’s positions. - Compensation/benefits (cb): - Join ebe to position_job_profile by position_id and source_relation, then to job_profile by job_profile_id and source_relation. - Read compensation_grade_id and classify compensation_tier by the first sequence of digits found in the grade id converted to a number: missing/non-numeric → ''Ungraded''; ≤5 → ''Entry Level''; ≤10 → ''Mid Level''; ≤15 → ''Senior Level''; >15 → ''Executive Level''. - work_conditions_score is the sum of three components (0–3): shift required → 0 else 1; union eligible → 1 else 0; default_weekly_hours ≤40 → 1 else 0; NULLs follow the else branch. - Build the comprehensive profile by left joining ebe to elm (worker_id, source_relation), cdc (worker_id, source_relation, position_id), oc (worker_id, source_relation), and cb (worker_id, source_relation, position_id). Compute: - age from date_of_birth relative to current_date with birthday adjustment; NULL when date_of_birth is NULL. - tenure_group from days_as_worker: <365 ''< 1 year''; <730 ''1-2 years''; <1825 ''2-5 years''; <3650 ''5-10 years''; else ''10+ years'' (NULL falls to else). - Default NULLs using these rules: - Lifecycle: retained_30_days/90_days/1_year/3_years → 0; churn_risk_category → ''Unknown''; employee_maturity_segment → ''Unknown''; departure_category → ''Active Employee''; tenure_years → 0; hire_quarter_cohort → ''Unknown''; hire_year_cohort → calendar year of hire_date; retention_stability_score → 0; hire_month → month number of hire_date; hire_season → ''Unknown''. - Career: total_positions_held → 1; total_promotions/demotions/lateral_moves/fast_promotions/management_positions_held → 0; highest_management_level_reached → 1; career_stability_category → ''Unknown''; career_growth_pace → ''Stable Career''; career_development_score → 0; career_phase → ''Unknown''. - Organization: organization_id/name/type/sub_type → ''Unknown''; dept_size → 0; dept_health_score → 0; dept_turnover_rate → 0; dept_management_ratio → 0; dept_performance_category → ''Unknown''; dept_size_category → ''Unknown''. - Compensation: compensation_grade_id → ''Ungraded''; compensation_tier → ''Ungraded''; is_work_shift_required/is_union_eligible → false; work_conditions_score → 0. - overall_employee_score = round(retention_stability_score*0.3 + career_development_score*0.4 + dept_health_score*0.2 + work_conditions_score*10*0.1, to 2 decimals); may fall outside 0–100. - employee_risk_level: - not employed → ''Former Employee'' - else: ''High Risk'' if churn risk is ''High Risk''; ''Medium-High Risk'' if churn risk is ''Medium Risk'' and career_development_score < 40; ''Medium Risk'' if churn risk is ''Medium Risk''; ''Medium-Low Risk'' if churn risk is ''Low Risk'' and dept_turnover_rate > 20; else ''Low Risk''. - employee_value_segment: - not employed → ''Former Employee'' - else: career_development_score ≥80 and retention_stability_score ≥80 → ''High Value - High Retention''; career_development_score ≥80 and retention_stability_score <80 → ''High Value - Flight Risk''; career_development_score <80 and retention_stability_score ≥80 → ''Stable - Development Needed''; career_development_score ≥60 → ''Moderate Value''; else ''Development Required''. - comprehensive_data_quality_flag (first match wins): invalid age (<16 or >80); future hire date; departure before hire; negative tenure; score out of range (<0 or >100); else ''VALID''. - analysis_timestamp is the current timestamp at execution.'
      columns:
      - name: employee_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: MD5 hash of '<worker_id>-<source_relation>-<position_id>-<position_start_date>' with '-' delimiter and '_dbt_utils_surrogate_key_null_' for NULLs; grouping key for the table
      - name: worker_id
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Worker identifier from int_workday__worker_employee_enhanced; passed through as-is (nullable)
      - name: source_relation
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Source system tag from int_workday__worker_employee_enhanced; passed through as-is (nullable)
      - name: position_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Position identifier from int_workday__worker_employee_enhanced; passed through as-is (nullable)
      - name: position_start_date
        data_type: DATE
        description: Position start date from int_workday__worker_employee_enhanced; used in employee_id hashing
      - name: worker_history_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Worker history identifier from int_workday__worker_employee_enhanced; passed through as-is
      - name: event_type
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Employment event type from int_workday__worker_employee_enhanced; passed through as-is
      - name: is_employed
        data_type: BOOLEAN
        description: Current employment status from int_workday__worker_employee_enhanced; drives 'Former Employee' classifications
      - name: hire_date
        data_type: DATE
        description: Original hire date; used for cohort defaults and data-quality checks
      - name: departure_date
        data_type: DATE
        description: Departure date if applicable; used in data-quality checks
      - name: days_as_worker
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Total days as worker from int_workday__worker_employee_enhanced; must be >= 0 to avoid error flag
      - name: first_name
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: First name from int_workday__worker_employee_enhanced; passed through as-is
      - name: last_name
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Last name from int_workday__worker_employee_enhanced; passed through as-is
      - name: full_name
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Full name from int_workday__worker_employee_enhanced; passed through as-is
      - name: date_of_birth
        data_type: DATE
        description: Date of birth; used to compute age relative to current_date
      - name: marital_status
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Marital status from int_workday__worker_employee_enhanced; passed through as-is
      - name: city_of_birth

        description: City of birth from int_workday__worker_employee_enhanced; passed through as-is

      - name: position_end_date
        data_type: DATE

      - name: days_in_position
        data_type: BIGINT

      - name: is_employed_one_year
        data_type: BOOLEAN
        description: Has been employed for at least one year; passed through from int_workday__worker_employee_enhanced
      - name: is_employed_five_years

      - name: is_employed_ten_years

      - name: is_current_employee_one_year
        data_type: BOOLEAN
        description: Current employee for at least one year; passed through from int_workday__worker_employee_enhanced
      - name: is_current_employee_five_years

        description: Current employee for at least five years; passed through from int_workday__worker_employee_enhanced
      - name: is_current_employee_ten_years
        data_type: BOOLEAN
        description: Current employee for at least ten years; passed through from int_workday__worker_employee_enhanced
      - name: age
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Age in years as of current_date using birthday adjustment; NULL if date_of_birth is NULL
      - name: tenure_group
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: 'Bucketed tenure from days_as_worker: <365 ''< 1 year''; <730 ''1-2 years''; <1825 ''2-5 years''; <3650 ''5-10 years''; else ''10+ years'' (NULL falls to ''10+ years'')'
      - name: retained_30_days
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Lifecycle retention flag; elm.retained_30_days with NULL → 0

        data_type: BIGINT
