      - name: highest_management_level_reached
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Largest recorded highest_management_level_reached per worker_id, source_relation, position_id with NULL → 1
      - name: career_stability_category
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Most frequent category per worker_id, source_relation, position_id; ties broken alphabetically; NULL → 'Unknown'
      - name: career_growth_pace
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Most frequent growth pace per worker_id, source_relation, position_id; ties broken alphabetically; NULL → 'Stable Career'
      - name: career_development_score
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Average career_development_score per worker_id, source_relation, position_id with NULL → 0
      - name: career_phase
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Most frequent phase per worker_id, source_relation, position_id; ties broken alphabetically; NULL → 'Unknown'
      - name: organization_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Current organization_id from join of worker_position_organization→organization_metrics; NULL → 'Unknown'
      - name: organization_name
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Current organization name; NULL → 'Unknown'
      - name: organization_type
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Organization type; NULL → 'Unknown'
      - name: organization_sub_type
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Organization sub-type; NULL → 'Unknown'
      - name: dept_size
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Department size (current_employee_count) with NULL → 0
      - name: dept_health_score
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Organization health score 0–100 with NULL → 0
      - name: dept_turnover_rate
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Annual turnover rate with NULL → 0; used in risk level ('Low Risk' + >20 → 'Medium-Low Risk')
      - name: dept_management_ratio
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Management ratio with NULL → 0
      - name: dept_performance_category
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Organization performance category with NULL → 'Unknown'
      - name: dept_size_category
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Organization size category with NULL → 'Unknown'
      - name: compensation_grade_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Job profile compensation grade id; NULL → 'Ungraded'
      - name: compensation_tier
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: 'Tier from the first digit sequence in compensation_grade_id: ≤5 ''Entry Level'', ≤10 ''Mid Level'', ≤15 ''Senior Level'', >15 ''Executive Level'', else ''Ungraded'''
      - name: is_work_shift_required
        data_type: BOOLEAN
        description: Job profile flag; final output NULL → false; contributes 0 if true else 1 to work_conditions_score
      - name: is_union_eligible
        data_type: BOOLEAN
        description: Job profile flag; final output NULL → false; contributes 1 if true else 0 to work_conditions_score
      - name: work_conditions_score
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: 'Sum of three components (range 0–3): not shift required=1, union eligible=1, default_weekly_hours≤40=1; computed with NULLs treated as false/0 and final NULL → 0'
      - name: overall_employee_score
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: 'Rounded to 2 decimals: retention_stability_score*0.3 + career_development_score*0.4 + dept_health_score*0.2 + work_conditions_score*10*0.1; not force-bounded to [0,100]'
      - name: employee_risk_level
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: 'Risk level: not employed ''Former Employee''; else High/Medium/Medium-High/Medium-Low/Low per churn risk, career_development_score<40, and dept_turnover_rate>20'
      - name: employee_value_segment
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: 'Value segment: not employed ''Former Employee''; else rules on career_development_score and retention_stability_score'
      - name: comprehensive_data_quality_flag
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: 'First-match checks: invalid age, future hire date, departure before hire, negative tenure, score out of range; else ''VALID'''
      - name: analysis_timestamp
        data_type: TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE
        description: Current timestamp at query execution time for the record build


## workday__workforce_analytics

    - name: workday__workforce_analytics
      description: Enterprise workforce KPI snapshot from employee overview with distinct counts, filtered averages and sums, and 2-decimal rounded percentages; uses the current date and timestamp at execution
      grain: One row per analysis_date (current_date) for the entire organization
      source_models:
      - mart.workday__employee_overview
      business_logic: 'Build three metric sets from mart.workday__employee_overview and cross join them to produce a single enterprise snapshot: - workforce_base_metrics: - Set source_relation to ''workday'' and analysis_date to the execution date. - Employee counts use distinct worker_id; position count uses distinct position_id. - Active vs former employees are split by the employment status flag. - Employee type counts (Regular, Contingent, Intern) are limited to active employees. - Weekly hours: - Average weekly hours are computed over active employees, excluding NULL values. - Total weekly hours are the sum over active employees, excluding NULL values. - Part-time counts include active employees with weekly hours below 40; full-time counts include active employees with weekly hours at or above 40; rows with NULL hours do not meet either predicate. - Diversity: - Ethnic diversity counts the distinct ethnicity code values among active employees; NULL codes are ignored. - Military veterans count distinct active workers with the military service flag true. - Age and generation groups among active employees: - Average age excludes NULL values. - Generation bands: 18–25 (Gen Z), 26–41 (Millennials), 42–57 (Gen X), and 58+ (Boomers). - retention_turnover_analysis: - Retention rates at 30 days, 90 days, 1 year, and 3 years are the mean of the corresponding retained flags among active employees, expressed as percentages and rounded to two decimals; if there are no active rows or all flags are NULL, the rate is NULL. - Churn risk distribution counts (High Risk, Medium Risk, Low Risk, Stable) use distinct worker_id across all rows without an employment filter. - Departure counts use distinct worker_id by departure_category (Early Termination, Short-term Departure, Mid-term Departure, Long-term Departure). - Annual turnover rate is the percentage of distinct workers who are no longer employed and departed in the past 12 months divided by distinct workers hired on or before one year ago, rounded to two decimals; if the denominator is zero, the value is NULL. - Tenure: - Average tenure for active employees converts average days as worker to years and rounds to two decimals; excludes NULL days. - Average tenure for departed employees applies the same conversion and rounding; excludes NULL days. - career_performance_metrics (filtered to active employees): - Average career development score is rounded to two decimals; NULL scores are excluded. - High achievers have at least two total promotions; fast track employees have one or more fast promotions. - Management levels: directors and above (level ≥ 4), senior managers (level = 3), managers (level = 2), individual contributors (level = 1). - Career phases use exact matches: Early Career, Mid Career, Senior Career. - Employee value segments use exact matches: High Value - High Retention, High Value - Flight Risk, Stable - Development Needed, Development Required. - Final computations: - Percentages divide the numerator by the stated denominator, multiply by 100, and round to two decimals; if the denominator is zero, the result is NULL. Specifically: - Position utilization = active employees over total positions. - Regular employee share = regular employees over active employees. - Full-time share = full-time employees over active employees. - Veteran share = military veterans over active employees. - High-risk share = high-risk employees over active employees. - High achiever share = high achievers over active employees. - Management share = (directors and above + senior managers + managers) over active employees. - Workforce health score (0–100) is the rounded sum of: - Retention component: 25 if the 1-year retention rate is at least 80; otherwise the rate is scaled by 0.3125. - Turnover component: 25 if the annual turnover rate is at most 15; otherwise subtract (annual turnover rate minus 15) × 1.67 from 25, floored at 0. - Career development component: 25 if the average career development score is at least 70; otherwise the score is scaled by 0.357. - Plus a fixed baseline of 25. - If any component is NULL, the overall score is NULL. - Workforce status flag precedence: 1) If there are no active employees, set to "ERROR: No active employees". 2) Else if annual turnover exceeds 50, set to "WARNING: High turnover rate". 3) Else if average career development score is below 30, set to "WARNING: Low career development". 4) Else set to "HEALTHY". - Analysis timestamp is captured at execution.'
      columns:
      - name: source_relation
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Constant string 'workday' identifying the source system for this snapshot
      - name: analysis_date
        data_type: DATE
        description: Date of the snapshot set to the execution day
      - name: active_employees
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct count of workers with employment status true
      - name: former_employees
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct count of workers with employment status false
      - name: total_employees_all_time
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct count of all workers present in the source
      - name: total_positions
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct count of positions across all rows
      - name: position_utilization_rate
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Active employees divided by total positions, multiplied by 100 and rounded to two decimals; NULL when total_positions is zero
      - name: regular_employees
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct count of active workers with employee_type 'Regular'
      - name: contingent_employees
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct count of active workers with employee_type 'Contingent'
      - name: intern_employees
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct count of active workers with employee_type 'Intern'
      - name: regular_employee_percentage
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Regular employees divided by active employees, multiplied by 100 and rounded to two decimals; NULL when active_employees is zero
      - name: avg_weekly_hours
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Average weekly hours among active employees excluding NULL values; units hours per week; not rounded
      - name: total_weekly_hours
        data_type: HUGEINT
        description: Total weekly hours summed among active employees excluding NULL values; units hours per week
      - name: part_time_employees
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct count of active workers with weekly hours below 40; rows with NULL hours do not qualify
      - name: full_time_employees
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct count of active workers with weekly hours at or above 40; rows with NULL hours do not qualify
      - name: full_time_percentage
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Full-time employees divided by active employees, multiplied by 100 and rounded to two decimals; NULL when active_employees is zero
      - name: ethnic_diversity_count
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct count of ethnicity codes among active employees; NULL codes excluded
      - name: military_veterans
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct count of active workers with military service indicated
      - name: veteran_percentage
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Military veterans divided by active employees, multiplied by 100 and rounded to two decimals; NULL when active_employees is zero
      - name: avg_employee_age
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Average age among active employees excluding NULL values; units years; not rounded
      - name: gen_z_employees
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct count of active workers aged 18–25 inclusive
      - name: millennial_employees
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct count of active workers aged 26–41 inclusive
      - name: gen_x_employees
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct count of active workers aged 42–57 inclusive
      - name: boomer_employees
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct count of active workers aged 58 or above
      - name: retention_rate_30_days
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Share of active workers retained 30 days expressed as a percentage and rounded to two decimals; NULL if no active rows or all flags are NULL
      - name: retention_rate_90_days
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Share of active workers retained 90 days expressed as a percentage and rounded to two decimals; NULL if no active rows or all flags are NULL
      - name: retention_rate_1_year
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Share of active workers retained one year expressed as a percentage and rounded to two decimals; NULL if no active rows or all flags are NULL
      - name: retention_rate_3_years
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Share of active workers retained three years expressed as a percentage and rounded to two decimals; NULL if no active rows or all flags are NULL
      - name: annual_turnover_rate
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Departures in the past 12 months divided by workers hired on or before one year ago, multiplied by 100 and rounded to two decimals; NULL if the denominator is zero
      - name: avg_tenure_years
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Average days as worker for active employees converted to years and rounded to two decimals; NULL if no valid values
      - name: avg_tenure_departed_years
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Average days as worker for departed employees converted to years and rounded to two decimals; NULL if no valid values
      - name: high_risk_employees
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct count of workers labeled 'High Risk' without an employment filter
      - name: medium_risk_employees

        description: Distinct count of workers labeled 'Medium Risk' without an employment filter
      - name: low_risk_employees
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct count of workers labeled 'Low Risk' without an employment filter
      - name: stable_employees
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct count of workers labeled 'Stable' without an employment filter
      - name: high_risk_percentage
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: High-risk employees divided by active employees, multiplied by 100 and rounded to two decimals; NULL when active_employees is zero
      - name: early_terminations
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct count of workers with departure_category 'Early Termination'
      - name: short_term_departures
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct count of workers with departure_category 'Short-term Departure'
      - name: mid_term_departures
        data_type: BIGINT

        description: Distinct count of workers with departure_category 'Long-term Departure'
      - name: avg_career_development_score
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Average career development score among active employees rounded to two decimals; NULL scores excluded
      - name: high_achievers
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct count of active workers with at least two total promotions
      - name: fast_track_employees
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct count of active workers with one or more fast promotions
      - name: high_achiever_percentage
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: High achievers divided by active employees, multiplied by 100 and rounded to two decimals; NULL when active_employees is zero
      - name: directors_and_above
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct count of active workers with highest management level at least 4
      - name: senior_managers

      - name: managers
        data_type: BIGINT

      - name: early_career_employees

      - name: workforce_status_flag

## workday__organization_performance

    - name: workday__organization_performance
      description: Department-level performance at the org level combining Workday org metrics with employee distributions, derived ratios, scores, risk levels, and recommendations
      grain: One row per (organization_id, source_relation) with current_employee_count > 0
      source_models:
      - intermediate.int_workday__organization_metrics
      - mart.workday__employee_overview
      business_logic: 'Build organization-level performance by: - Start with intermediate.int_workday__organization_metrics (om) and derive: - avg_weekly_hours_per_employee: when current employees exist, divide total weekly hours by current employees and round to 2 decimals; else 0; if total weekly hours is missing, the result is null. - span_of_control: when individual contributors > 0, divide individual contributors by the sum of managers, senior managers, and directors and round to 2 decimals; if that sum is 0 or missing, the result is null; if individual contributors = 0, the result is 0. - organization_maturity from avg_tenure_days: ≥ 3 years → "Mature Organization"; ≥ 2 years → "Developing Organization"; ≥ 1 year → "Growing Organization"; else "New Organization". - talent_density_ratio: when current employees exist, divide unique compensation grades by current employees, multiply by 100, and round to 2 decimals; else 0; if grades are missing, the result is null. - Build employee_performance_by_org from mart.workday__employee_overview excluding organization_id = ''Unknown'', grouped by (organization_id, source_relation): - Distinct worker_id counts by employee_value_segment: High Value - High Retention, High Value - Flight Risk, Stable - Development Needed, Development Required. - Career development: average career_development_score for employed workers rounded to 2 decimals; counts of distinct workers with total_promotions ≥ 2 (high achievers) and with fast_promotions > 0 (fast track), both only for employed. - Risk: distinct worker_id counts by churn_risk_category (High, Medium; Low includes "Low Risk" or "Stable"). - Satisfaction proxy: average overall_employee_score for employed workers rounded to 2 decimals. - Diversity: count of distinct non-null ethnicity codes for employed workers; count of distinct employed military service workers. - Age: average age for employed workers rounded to 1 decimal; cohort counts for employed workers: 18–30, 31–45, 46–60, and >60. - Left join org metrics to employee_performance_by_org on (organization_id, source_relation). - Compute additional org-level metrics: - regular_employee_percentage: regular employees divided by current employees multiplied by 100 and rounded to 2 decimals; null when regular employees is missing. - avg_tenure_years: avg_tenure_days divided by 365 and rounded to 2 decimals; null when avg_tenure_days is missing. - Performance rates (high_value_retention_rate, talent_flight_risk_rate, high_achiever_percentage, high_risk_percentage): each count divided by current employees multiplied by 100 and rounded to 2 decimals; counts default to 0 when missing. - Satisfaction, diversity, veterans, age metrics: averages and counts default to 0 when missing; averages rounded as specified. - Enhanced organization effectiveness score (0–100): weighted sum rounded to 2 decimals and always non-null: - Talent quality (30%): avg_career_development_score scaled by 0.3 and capped at 30. - Retention stability (25%): 25/20/15/10 based on annual_turnover_rate thresholds (≤10, ≤20, ≤30, >30). - Satisfaction (20%): avg_employee_satisfaction_proxy scaled by 0.2 and capped at 20. - Management efficiency (15%): 15 when span_of_control is 5–12; 10 when 3–15; else 5. - Baseline: 10 points. - Organization risk level (precedence): - Critical Risk if annual_turnover_rate > 40. - High Risk if annual_turnover_rate > 25 or high_risk_employees exceed 30% of current employees. - Medium Risk if annual_turnover_rate > 15 or high_risk_employees exceed 20% of current employees. - Low Risk if annual_turnover_rate ≤ 10 and avg_career_development_score ≥ 70. - Otherwise Moderate Risk. - Recommended action (precedence): - Focus on Retention Strategy if annual_turnover_rate > 25. - Enhance Career Development Programs if avg_career_development_score < 50. - Optimize Management Structure if management_ratio > 30. - Improve Employee Experience if avg_employee_satisfaction_proxy < 60. - Otherwise Maintain Current Excellence. - Data quality flag (precedence): - ERROR: No employees in organization if current_employee_count = 0. - ERROR: Turnover rate exceeds 100% if annual_turnover_rate > 100. - WARNING: Extremely high management ratio if management_ratio > 80. - WARNING: No career development data if avg_career_development_score = 0 and current_employee_count > 0. - Otherwise VALID. - Filter: include only rows with current_employee_count > 0. - Order: sort by enhanced_organization_effectiveness_score descending, then current_employee_count descending. - analysis_timestamp: set to the current execution time (time zone aware).'
      columns:
      - name: organization_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Organizational unit identifier and join key to employee overview; grain is per organization_id and source_relation
      - name: source_relation
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Source system identifier used for grouping and joining
      - name: organization_name
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Organization display name from org metrics
      - name: organization_code
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Organization code from org metrics
      - name: organization_type
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Organization type from org metrics
      - name: organization_sub_type
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Organization sub-type from org metrics
      - name: staffing_model
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Staffing model from org metrics
      - name: current_employee_count
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Active employee count from org metrics; final output includes only rows where this value > 0
      - name: total_positions
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Total positions from org metrics
      - name: position_fill_rate
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Position fill rate from org metrics (percentage scale as provided)
      - name: organization_size_category
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Size category from org metrics
      - name: regular_employees
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Regular employees count from org metrics
      - name: contingent_employees
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Contingent employees count from org metrics
      - name: intern_employees
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Intern employees count from org metrics
      - name: regular_employee_percentage
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Share of regular employees among current employees multiplied by 100, rounded to 2 decimals; null when regular_employees is missing
      - name: avg_weekly_hours
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Average weekly hours from org metrics

      - name: managers_count

      - name: senior_managers_count

      - name: directors_count

        description: Directors count from org metrics

      - name: organization_maturity

        description: Most frequent compensation grade from org metrics

      - name: talent_density_ratio

        description: Unique compensation grades divided by current employees multiplied by 100, rounded to 2 decimals; 0 when no current employees; null when grades are missing
      - name: high_value_retained_count
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct worker_id count with employee_value_segment = 'High Value - High Retention'; defaults to 0 when missing
      - name: high_value_flight_risk_count
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct worker_id count with employee_value_segment = 'High Value - Flight Risk'; defaults to 0 when missing
      - name: stable_development_count
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct worker_id count with employee_value_segment = 'Stable - Development Needed'; defaults to 0 when missing
      - name: development_required_count
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct worker_id count with employee_value_segment = 'Development Required'; defaults to 0 when missing
      - name: high_value_retention_rate
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Share of current employees in 'High Value - High Retention' multiplied by 100 and rounded to 2 decimals; denominator is current_employee_count (>0)
      - name: talent_flight_risk_rate
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Share of current employees in 'High Value - Flight Risk' multiplied by 100 and rounded to 2 decimals; denominator is current_employee_count (>0)
      - name: avg_career_development_score
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Average career development score for employed workers rounded to 2 decimals; defaults to 0 when no data
      - name: high_achievers_count
        data_type: BIGINT

      - name: fast_track_count
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct employed workers with fast_promotions > 0; defaults to 0 when missing
      - name: high_achiever_percentage
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Share of current employees who are high achievers multiplied by 100 and rounded to 2 decimals; denominator is current_employee_count (>0)
      - name: high_risk_employees
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct worker_id with churn_risk_category = 'High Risk'; defaults to 0 when missing
      - name: medium_risk_employees
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct worker_id with churn_risk_category = 'Medium Risk'; defaults to 0 when missing
      - name: low_risk_employees
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct worker_id with churn_risk_category in 'Low Risk' or 'Stable'; defaults to 0 when missing
      - name: high_risk_percentage
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Share of current employees in 'High Risk' multiplied by 100 and rounded to 2 decimals; denominator is current_employee_count (>0)
      - name: avg_employee_satisfaction_proxy
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Average overall employee score for employed workers rounded to 2 decimals; defaults to 0 when no data
      - name: ethnic_diversity_count
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct non-null ethnicity codes among employed workers; defaults to 0
      - name: military_veterans_count
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct employed workers with military service; defaults to 0
      - name: avg_employee_age
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Average age among employed workers rounded to 1 decimal; defaults to 0 when no data
      - name: young_professionals_count
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct employed workers aged 18–30; defaults to 0
      - name: mid_career_professionals_count
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct employed workers aged 31–45; defaults to 0
      - name: senior_professionals_count
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct employed workers aged 46–60; defaults to 0
      - name: pre_retirement_professionals_count
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct employed workers aged >60; defaults to 0
      - name: organization_health_score
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Composite health score from org metrics
      - name: performance_category
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Performance category label from org metrics
      - name: enhanced_organization_effectiveness_score
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Weighted score (0–100) combining talent (capped at 30), retention (10–25), satisfaction (capped at 20), management efficiency (5/10/15), plus baseline 10; rounded to 2 decimals and non-null
      - name: organization_risk_level
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: 'Risk classification by precedence: Critical if turnover >40%; High if turnover >25% or high_risk employees >30% of current employees; Medium if turnover >15% or high_risk >20%; Low if turnover ≤10% and career development ≥70; else Moderate'
      - name: recommended_action
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: 'Primary recommendation by precedence: high turnover → retention; low career development → develop programs; high management_ratio → optimize; low satisfaction → improve; else maintain'
      - name: data_quality_flag
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: 'Quality flag by precedence: zero employees, turnover >100%, management_ratio >80, no career development data, else VALID'
      - name: analysis_timestamp
        data_type: TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE
        description: Execution timestamp set at query time (time zone aware)

## workday__organization_overview

    - name: workday__organization_overview
      description: Executive-ready overview of each active organization with headcount, roles, tenure, mobility, risk, performance, diversity, compensation tiers, promotions, derived scores, recommendations, and data quality
      grain: One row per active organization_id and source_relation
      source_models:
      - staging.stg_workday__organization (filtered to is_inactive = false)
      - staging.stg_workday__worker_position_organization
      - staging.stg_workday__organization_role_worker
      - staging.stg_workday__organization_role
      - mart.workday__employee_overview (filtered to organization_id != 'Unknown')
      business_logic: '1) Filter organizations to active only and expose attributes and effective_start as the creation date. 2) Build worker–organization–role relationships by left joining worker positions to role-worker and role master on matching worker, role, source_relation, and organization_id. For each assignment: - assignment_status: ''Active Assignment'' if end date is null, else ''Former Assignment''. - assignment_duration_days: days between start and end; if end is null, days between start and current_date. - carry organization_role_id and organization_role_code from the role join. 3) Aggregate organization_employee_stats by (organization_id, source_relation): - total_employees_ever: distinct workers across all assignments. - current_active_employees: distinct workers with Active Assignment. - former_employees: distinct workers with Former Assignment. - total_positions_in_org: distinct positions across all assignments. - active_positions_count: distinct positions with Active Assignment. - new_assignments_last_year: distinct workers with start date in the last year relative to current_date. - departures_last_year: distinct workers with a non-null end date in the last year. - avg_current_tenure_days: average assignment_duration_days for Active Assignments. - avg_former_tenure_days: average assignment_duration_days for Former Assignments. - unique_organization_roles: distinct organization_role_id. - management_role_employees / director_role_employees / lead_role_employees: distinct workers where role code contains ''Manager'' / ''Director'' / ''Lead''. - last_new_assignment_date: most recent start date; last_departure_date: most recent end date. 4) Aggregate organization_performance_metrics from mart.workday__employee_overview by (organization_id, source_relation), excluding organization_id = ''Unknown'': - Averages over currently employed workers only: overall_employee_score and career_development_score rounded to 2 decimals; retention_stability_score rounded to 2 decimals; age rounded to 1 decimal. - Employee value segments: distinct workers by segment (''High Value - High Retention'', ''High Value - Flight Risk'', ''Stable - Development Needed'', ''Development Required''); no employment filter. - Risk distribution: distinct workers by employee_risk_level (High/Medium/Low); no employment filter. - Maturity segments: distinct employed workers where maturity contains ''New Hire'' / ''Junior'' / ''Experienced'' / ''Senior'' / ''Veteran''. - Diversity: distinct ethnicity_codes among employed; military_veterans_count is distinct employed workers flagged for military service. - Compensation tiers: distinct employed workers by compensation_tier (Entry/Mid/Senior/Executive Level). - Promotions among employed: total_promotions summed; high_achievers_count is distinct workers with total_promotions ≥ 2; fast_track_employees_count is distinct workers with fast_promotions > 0. 5) Derive organization attributes per active organization: - organization_created_date from effective_start. - organization_age_days as days from creation to current_date; organization_age_years is that divided by 365, rounded to 2 decimals. - organization_maturity_level by age_days: <1 year New; 1–3 years Young; 3–10 years Mature; 10+ years Legacy. - organizational_level from organization_type: Company/Division/Department/Team mapped to corresponding Level; others to Other Level. - access_level from visibility: Public→Open Access, Private→Restricted Access, others Unknown Access. 6) Final metrics and rules (rates rounded to 2 decimals; missing metrics default to 0 unless noted): - position_fill_rate (%) = current_active_employees ÷ total_positions × 100 when total_positions > 0, else 0. - annual_turnover_rate (%) = departures_last_year ÷ current_active_employees × 100 when current_active_employees > 0, else 0. - avg_current_tenure_years and avg_former_tenure_years = respective average tenure days ÷ 365, rounded to 2; if no data, treated as 0. - management_ratio (%) = (management_role_employees + director_role_employees) ÷ current_active_employees × 100 when current_active_employees > 0, else 0. - high_risk_employee_percentage (%) = high_risk_employees ÷ current_active_employees × 100 when current_active_employees > 0, else 0. - organization_health_score (0–100) = sum of: performance (cap at 30% of average performance), turnover (25/20/15/10/5 by thresholds using annual_turnover_rate and zero departures case), development (cap at 20% of average career development), efficiency (15/12/9/6 by position_fill_rate thresholds), and risk (10/7/4/2 by high_risk_employee_percentage thresholds); final score rounded to 2 decimals. - performance_category uses a weighted composite with thresholds: Excellent if composite ≥ 80; else Good if ≥ 60; else Average if ≥ 40; otherwise Needs Improvement (weights and constants follow the detailed rules above). - organization_size_category by current_active_employees: 0 Empty; ≤5 Micro; ≤15 Small; ≤50 Medium; ≤200 Large; else Enterprise. - last_activity_date is the most recent assignment start; if none, use organization_created_date. - last_departure_date is the most recent assignment end; if none, use 1900-01-01. - management_recommendation (first match): if no active employees then review necessity; else if turnover > 30% then focus on retention; else if high-risk % > 25% then address risk; else if fill rate < 70% then improve staffing; else if average performance < 50 then enhance performance management; else if management_ratio > 40% then optimize structure; else maintain excellence. - data_quality_flag precedence: missing or blank organization name → ERROR; active employees exceed positions by more than 10 → WARNING; turnover rate > 100% → WARNING; negative age days → ERROR; otherwise VALID. - analysis_timestamp is the query execution timestamp. 7) Join logic: left join employee stats and performance metrics to active organizations on (organization_id, source_relation); missing measures default to 0. The final ordering is for presentation only and does not affect grain.'
      columns:
      - name: organization_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Organization identifier; join key to stats and metrics; one output row per active (organization_id, source_relation)
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: source_relation
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Source system code carried from staging and used in all joins
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: organization_name
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Organization display name from the active organization record
      - name: organization_code
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Organization code from the active organization record

        description: Visibility setting from source; drives access_level mapping
      - name: access_level

      - name: organization_created_date
        data_type: DATE
        description: Effective start date of the active organization record
      - name: organization_age_days
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Days since organization_created_date to current_date at run time
      - name: organization_age_years
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Rounded years since creation = round(organization_age_days/365.0, 2)
      - name: organization_maturity_level
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: 'Age bucket: <1y New; 1–3y Young; 3–10y Mature; 10+y Legacy based on organization_age_days'
      - name: organizational_level
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: 'Level mapped from organization_type: Company/Division/Department/Team→Corporate/Division/Department/Team Level; else Other Level'
      - name: total_employees_ever
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct workers ever assigned to the org; defaults to 0 when missing
      - name: current_active_employees
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct workers with Active Assignment at run time; defaults to 0 when missing
      - name: former_employees
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct workers with Former Assignment; defaults to 0 when missing
      - name: total_positions

        description: Distinct positions ever assigned within the org; defaults to 0 when missing

## workday__job_overview

    - name: workday__job_overview
      description: Job profile overview aggregated from Workday staging tables with usage, employee, career mobility, market value, and health metrics
      grain: One row per job_profile_id and source_relation
      source_models:
      - staging.stg_workday__job_profile
      - staging.stg_workday__job_family_job_profile
      - staging.stg_workday__job_family
      - staging.stg_workday__job_family_job_family_group
      - staging.stg_workday__job_family_group
      - staging.stg_workday__position_job_profile
      - staging.stg_workday__position
      - staging.stg_workday__worker_position_organization
      - mart.workday__employee_overview
      business_logic: 'Construct one row per job_profile_id and source_relation: - Base attributes: take all master job profile fields. - Job family hierarchy: - Link each profile to its job families and family groups through the provided relationships. - For each profile, collect distinct job family codes and names, and distinct family group codes and names; concatenate with ", "; exclude nulls; no enforced sort order. Also count distinct families and distinct family groups. - Position utilization: - Link positions to the profile, then link worker-position-organization assignments. - Positions: total is the number of distinct positions linked; active positions are those with at least one current assignment; inactive positions are those with at least one ended assignment (a position can appear in both if it has both current and ended assignments). - Employees: current is distinct workers on current assignments; ever is distinct workers on any assignment. - Weekly hours: compute average, minimum, and maximum of position default weekly hours (no rounding). - Employee types: for current assignments, count distinct workers by position employee_type (Regular, Contingent, Intern). - Organizations: count distinct organization IDs using the job; the primary organization is the most frequent ID, with ties resolved by the lowest ID when sorted ascending. - Career mobility and demographics (from employee overview joined via position-job profile): - Averages: career development score (for employed workers, rounded to 2 decimals) and age (for employed workers, rounded to 1 decimal). - High achievers: distinct employed workers with two or more promotions. - Fast track: distinct employed workers with any fast promotion. - Retention risk: distinct workers by churn risk category (High, Medium, and Low/Stable) without restricting to employed. - Maturity segments: distinct employed workers in segments matching New Hire, Junior, Experienced, Senior, Veteran. - Age bands: distinct employed workers aged 18–30, 31–45, and 46–60 (inclusive ranges). - Compensation tiers: distinct employed workers in Entry, Mid, Senior, Executive tiers. - Market value: - Job rarity: categorize by total positions (0 → No Positions; ≤2 → Very Rare; ≤5 → Rare; ≤15 → Uncommon; ≤50 → Common; >50 → Very Common). - Talent competition: if there are no current employees, label No Current Employees; otherwise compute current employees per position and assign High (≥0.9), Moderate (≥0.7), Low (≥0.5), or Very Low. - Job maturity: categorize by average career score (≥80 Highly Developed Role; ≥60 Well Developed Role; ≥40 Moderately Developed Role; ≥20 Developing Role; else New/Emerging Role). - Retention difficulty: if there are no current employees, label No Data; otherwise compute the share of high-risk workers among current and assign High (≥0.3), Moderate (≥0.2), Low (≥0.1), or Excellent. - Percentages: high performer percentage = 100 × (high achievers / current employees), and career growth potential percentage = 100 × (fast track / current employees); when there are no current employees, both are 0; round both to 2 decimals. - Defaults in the final output when values are missing: - Job family strings → "Unknown"; counts → 0. - Weekly hours: average → 40; minimum and maximum → 0. - Organization metrics: organizations_using_job_count → 0; primary_organization_id → "Unknown". - Career averages: avg_career_development_score → 0; avg_employee_age → 0. - Market labels: job_rarity_level, talent_competition_level, job_maturity_level, retention_difficulty_level → "Unknown". - Percentages: high_performer_percentage and career_growth_potential_percentage → 0. - Job health score (rounded to 2 decimals) is the sum of: - +20 if there are current employees, else +0. - Career development: +25 if average career score ≥70; otherwise average career score × 0.357. - High performers: +25 if high performer percentage ≥30; otherwise high performer percentage × 0.833. - Retention stability: +15 if there are no high-risk workers; +10 if the high-risk share among current employees is ≤10%; otherwise +5. - Organizational breadth: +15 if three or more organizations use the job; otherwise 5 points per organization. - Job value tier: - If there are no current employees → Inactive Job. - Else, evaluate in order: (1) high performer percentage ≥40 and average career score ≥80 → Strategic High-Value Role; (2) high performer percentage ≥25 and average career score ≥60 → High-Value Role; (3) high performer percentage ≥15 and average career score ≥40 → Standard Value Role; (4) high performer percentage ≥10 → Developing Role; else Entry-Level Role. - Talent management recommendation (evaluate in order): - No current employees → Review Job Necessity. - High-risk share among current employees >30% → Focus on Retention. - High performer percentage <15% → Enhance Performance Management. - Average career score <50 → Improve Career Development. - Exactly one organization uses the job → Consider Cross-Organizational Expansion. - Else → Maintain Excellence. - Data quality flag (evaluate in order): - Missing or blank job title → ERROR: Missing job title. - Negative total position count → ERROR: Negative position count. - Current employees exceed total positions → WARNING: More employees than positions. - Average weekly hours >80 → WARNING: Excessive working hours. - Else → VALID. - Row ordering in the final result: sort by current_employees_count descending, then high_performer_percentage descending, then job_title ascending.'
      columns:
      - name: job_profile_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Job profile identifier used as a grouping key; one row per job_profile_id+source_relation
        tests:
        - not_null
        - unique
      - name: source_relation
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Source system identifier paired with job_profile_id for joins and grouping
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: job_profile_code
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Workday job profile code from the master profile
      - name: job_title
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Job profile title from the master profile
      - name: job_summary
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Job profile summary from the master profile
      - name: management_level
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Management level associated with the job profile
      - name: compensation_grade_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Compensation grade identifier from the job profile
      - name: is_work_shift_required
        data_type: BOOLEAN
        description: Indicates whether the job requires work shifts per the master profile
      - name: is_union_eligible
        data_type: BOOLEAN
        description: Indicates whether the job is union eligible per the master profile
      - name: job_family_codes
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Distinct job family codes concatenated with ', '; nulls excluded; order not enforced; default 'Unknown'
      - name: job_family_names
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Distinct job family names concatenated with ', '; nulls excluded; order not enforced; default 'Unknown'
      - name: job_family_group_codes
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Distinct job family group codes concatenated with ', '; nulls excluded; order not enforced; default 'Unknown'
      - name: job_family_group_names
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Distinct job family group names concatenated with ', '; nulls excluded; order not enforced; default 'Unknown'
      - name: associated_job_families_count
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Count of distinct job families linked to the profile; default 0
      - name: associated_job_family_groups_count
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Count of distinct job family groups linked via families; default 0
      - name: total_positions_count
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct positions associated with the job profile; default 0
      - name: active_positions_count
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct positions with at least one current assignment; default 0
      - name: inactive_positions_count
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct positions with at least one ended assignment; default 0
      - name: current_employees_count
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct workers on current assignments for the job; default 0
      - name: total_employees_ever_count
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct workers ever assigned to positions for the job; default 0
      - name: avg_weekly_hours
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Average of position default weekly hours (no rounding); default 40 when missing
      - name: min_weekly_hours
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Minimum position default weekly hours; default 0 when missing
      - name: max_weekly_hours
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Maximum position default weekly hours; default 0 when missing
      - name: regular_employees_count
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct current workers where position employee_type is 'Regular'; default 0
      - name: contingent_employees_count
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct current workers where position employee_type is 'Contingent'; default 0
      - name: intern_employees_count
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct current workers where position employee_type is 'Intern'; default 0
      - name: organizations_using_job_count
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct organization IDs using the job across assignments; default 0
      - name: primary_organization_id
        data_type: VARCHAR

## stg_workday__position_organization

    - name: stg_workday__position_organization
      description: "Association table linking positions to organizations with effective dates"
      source_table: "raw.position_organization"
      columns:
        - name: source_relation
          data_type: VARCHAR
          constraints: [not_null]
        - name: position_id
          data_type: VARCHAR
        - name: organization_id
          data_type: VARCHAR
        - name: effective_start
          data_type: DATE
        - name: effective_end
          data_type: DATE



## workday__position_overview

    - name: workday__position_overview
      description: Position-level overview with occupancy history, organizational context, compensation/job profile attributes, current-employee metrics, and derived scores/flags
      grain: One row per position_id and source_relation
      source_models:
      - staging.stg_workday__position
      - staging.stg_workday__worker_position
      - staging.stg_workday__position_organization
      - staging.stg_workday__organization
      - intermediate.int_workday__organization_metrics
      - staging.stg_workday__position_job_profile
      - staging.stg_workday__job_profile
      - mart.workday__employee_overview
      business_logic: '- Base: Start from positions. Compute position_age_days as the days from effective_start to today. Bucket position_age_category by age: <90 days "New Position (<3 months)"; <365 days "Recent Position (3-12 months)"; <2 years "Established Position (1-2 years)"; <5 years "Mature Position (2-5 years)"; otherwise "Legacy Position (5+ years)". - Utilization history: Join worker-position assignments by position and source. Count distinct workers ever assigned (total), with open end date (current), and with closed end date (former). Set position_occupancy_status to "Occupied" if current>0; else "Vacant (Previously Occupied)" if total>0; else "Vacant (Never Occupied)". Average tenure days uses assignment start-to-end when ended, or start-to-today when still open. Take the most recent assignment start as last_assignment_date and most recent end as last_departure_date. New assignments last year count distinct workers with assignment start within the past 12 months; departures last year count distinct workers with assignment end within the past 12 months. - Organizational context: Link positions to organizations and organization metrics. Also link to job profile. similar_positions_in_department is the count of positions sharing the same organization_id and job_profile_id across the dataset. - Compensation and job profile: Derive compensation_tier from the first number found in compensation_grade_id: missing or not numeric → "Ungraded"; ≤5 → "Entry Level"; ≤10 → "Mid Level"; ≤15 → "Senior Level"; >15 → "Executive Level". challenging_work_conditions_score is the sum of three indicators (shift required, union eligible, weekly hours>40), range 0–3. Map management_level to a numeric scale: Director 4, Senior Manager 3, Manager 2, Individual Contributor 1, otherwise 0. - Current employee metrics: From employee overview by position and source. Averages (overall performance, career development, retention stability) are computed only for currently employed workers. Risk counts (High/Medium/Low Risk) are distinct worker counts by risk level without restricting to current employment. Value segments (High Value - High Retention, High Value - Flight Risk) are distinct worker counts by segment. New and veteran counts include only currently employed workers with maturity containing "New Hire" or "Veteran". Average age and ethnic diversity use only currently employed workers; military_veterans_count counts currently employed workers with military service. - Final projection and defaults: If any numeric metric is missing, default to 0; booleans default to false; text dimensions default to "Unknown"; compensation grade/tier default to "Ungraded". last_assignment_date falls back to position_created_date when no assignments; last_departure_date remains null when none. - Rounding: avg_tenure_years_in_position is average tenure days divided by 365, rounded to two decimals. Average employee scores are rounded to two decimals. Average employee age is rounded to one decimal. position_turnover_rate and position_efficiency_score are rounded to two decimals. - Derived metrics: position_turnover_rate is 0 if total ever assigned is 0; otherwise departures in the last year divided by total ever assigned, expressed as a percentage. position_efficiency_score (0–100) is the sum of: 25 if occupied; a tenure bucket (25 if tenure≥2 years, 20 if ≥1 year, 15 if ≥180 days, else 10); up to 30 points from employee performance (performance score × 0.3, capped at 30); up to 20 points from department health (health score × 0.2, capped at 20); then rounded. - Categorization rules (first-match wins): position_performance_category: Vacant Position; High-Performance Stable Position (performance≥80 and tenure≥2 years); High-Performance Position (performance≥70); Stable Position (tenure≥2 years); High-Turnover Position (departures_last_year>1); else Standard Position. management_priority: Review Position Necessity (vacant and age>180 days); Focus on Retention (any high risk employees); Performance Improvement Needed (performance<50); Department Support Needed (department health<50); Maintain Excellence (performance≥80); else Standard Management. cost_performance_analysis: No Current Cost (vacant); High Cost - Low Performance (weekly hours>40 and performance<60); High Cost - High Performance (weekly hours>40 and performance≥80); Optimal Cost-Performance (weekly hours≤40 and performance≥80); else Standard Cost-Performance. strategic_recommendation: Consider Position Elimination (vacant and age>365 days); Consider Position Consolidation (similar positions>5); Develop Succession Plan (any flight risk employees); Consider Position Expansion (performance≥90); else Maintain Current Structure. - Data quality: Mark as ERROR when title is missing/blank, weekly hours≤0, or position_age_days<0; mark as WARNING when weekly hours>80 or current assignments>3; otherwise VALID. - Output ordering: Sort rows by current_workers_assigned descending, then average employee performance descending, then position_age_days descending. - Analysis timestamp: Set to the current timestamp at query execution (time zone aware).'
      columns:
      - name: position_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Unique position identifier; primary key with source_relation; exactly one output row per (position_id, source_relation)
        tests:
        - not_null
        - unique
      - name: source_relation
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Source system identifier associated with the position_id
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: business_title
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Business title from position source; may be NULL/empty and is validated in data_quality_flag
      - name: employee_type
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Employee type as stored on the position record
      - name: default_weekly_hours
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Default scheduled weekly hours from position; used in cost and work-conditions scoring; may be <=0 or >80 (flagged in data_quality_flag)
      - name: position_created_date
        data_type: DATE
        description: Position effective_start date from the source, treated as the created date
      - name: position_age_days
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Days between effective_start and today; can be negative for future-dated positions
      - name: position_age_category
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: 'Age bucket based on position_age_days: <90 New; <365 Recent; <730 Established; <1825 Mature; else Legacy'
      - name: total_workers_ever_assigned
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct worker count ever assigned to the position; defaults to 0 when no history
        tests:
        - total_workers_ever_assigned >= 0
      - name: current_workers_assigned
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct worker count with an open assignment (no end date); defaults to 0
        tests:
        - current_workers_assigned >= 0
      - name: former_workers_assigned
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct worker count with a closed assignment (has end date); defaults to 0
        tests:
        - former_workers_assigned >= 0
      - name: position_occupancy_status
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Occupied if current_workers_assigned>0; else Vacant (Previously Occupied) if total>0; else Vacant (Never Occupied); defaults to 'Unknown' if missing
      - name: avg_tenure_years_in_position
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Average tenure in years based on assignment days/365, rounded to 2 decimals; open assignments use today; defaults to 0.00
        tests:
        - avg_tenure_years_in_position >= 0
      - name: last_assignment_date
        data_type: DATE
        description: Most recent assignment start date; falls back to position_created_date when none
      - name: last_departure_date
        data_type: DATE
        description: Most recent assignment end date; NULL when none
      - name: new_assignments_last_year
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct worker count with assignment start within the past 12 months; defaults to 0
        tests:
        - new_assignments_last_year >= 0
      - name: departures_last_year
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct worker count with assignment end within the past 12 months; defaults to 0
        tests:
        - departures_last_year >= 0
      - name: position_turnover_rate
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: If total_workers_ever_assigned=0 then 0; otherwise departures_last_year divided by total_workers_ever_assigned as a percentage, rounded to 2 decimals
        tests:
        - position_turnover_rate >= 0
      - name: organization_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Organization identifier for the position; defaults to 'Unknown' when absent
      - name: organization_name
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Organization name; defaults to 'Unknown' when absent
      - name: organization_type
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Organization type; defaults to 'Unknown' when absent
      - name: organization_sub_type
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Organization sub-type; defaults to 'Unknown' when absent
      - name: staffing_model
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Organization staffing model; defaults to 'Unknown' when absent
      - name: department_total_employees
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Current employee count for the organization from org metrics; defaults to 0
        tests:
        - department_total_employees >= 0
      - name: department_health_score
        data_type: INTEGER
        description: Organization health score (0–100) from org metrics; defaults to 0
        tests:
        - department_health_score BETWEEN 0 AND 100
      - name: department_turnover_rate
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Annual turnover rate from org metrics; defaults to 0.0
        tests:
        - department_turnover_rate >= 0
      - name: department_performance_level
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Organization performance category from org metrics; defaults to 'Unknown'
      - name: department_size_category

        description: Organization size category from org metrics; defaults to 'Unknown'

        tests:
        - similar_positions_in_department >= 0
      - name: job_profile_id
        data_type: VARCHAR

#!/usr/bin/env python3
import os
import yaml
import duckdb
from pathlib import Path
from typing import List, Optional
import logging


logging.basicConfig(
    level=logging.INFO,
    format='%(asctime)s - %(levelname)s - %(message)s'
)
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class WorkdayDataPipeline:
    """Workday Data Pipeline Orchestrator - Supports a layered Schema architecture"""
    
    def __init__(self, 
                 source_db_path: str = "workday_start.duckdb", 
                 target_db_path: str = "workday.duckdb",
                 config_path: str = "config/layer_dependencies.yaml"):
        self.source_db_path = source_db_path
        self.target_db_path = target_db_path
        self.config_path = config_path
        self.config = self._load_config()
        self.sql_dir = Path("sql")
        
                
        self.schema_config = {
            'raw': 'raw',                       
            'staging': 'staging',               
            'intermediate': 'intermediate',             
            'marts': 'mart'                      
        }
        
                
        self.conn = None
        
    def _load_config(self) -> dict:
        """Load the configuration file"""
        try:
            with open(self.config_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                return yaml.safe_load(f)
        except FileNotFoundError:
            logger.error(f"Configuration file {self.config_path} not found")
            raise
        except yaml.YAMLError as e:
            logger.error(f"Error parsing configuration file: {e}")
            raise
    
    def _initialize_database(self):
        """Initialize the working database, create the layered schema architecture, and import raw data"""
        logger.info(f"📥 Creating new layered database: {self.target_db_path}")
        
                
        if not os.path.exists(self.source_db_path):
            raise FileNotFoundError(f"Source database file not found: {self.source_db_path}")
        
        self.conn = duckdb.connect(self.target_db_path)
        
                
        self._create_schemas()
        
                
        self._import_raw_data()
        
        logger.info(f"✓ Layered database initialization complete: {self.target_db_path}")
    
    def _create_schemas(self):
        """Create schemas for data layers"""
        logger.info("🏗️ Creating Schema layered architecture")
        
        for layer, schema_name in self.schema_config.items():
            try:
                self.conn.execute(f"CREATE SCHEMA IF NOT EXISTS {schema_name}")
                logger.info(f"  ✓ Created schema: {schema_name}")
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"  ✗ Failed to create schema {schema_name}: {e}")
                raise
    
    def _import_raw_data(self):
        """Import raw data from the source database to the raw schema"""
        logger.info("📥 Importing raw data into the raw schema")
        
                
        try:
            source_conn = duckdb.connect(self.source_db_path)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Unable to connect to source database {self.source_db_path}: {e}")
            raise
        
        try:
            
            tables_result = source_conn.execute("SELECT table_name FROM information_schema.tables WHERE table_schema = 'raw'").fetchall()
            source_tables = [table[0] for table in tables_result]
            
            logger.info(f"Found {len(source_tables)} raw tables")
            
                        
            for table_name in source_tables:
                try:
                    
                    data = source_conn.execute(f"SELECT * FROM raw.{table_name}").fetchall()
                    columns_info = source_conn.execute(f"DESCRIBE raw.{table_name}").fetchall()
                    
                                        
                    column_defs = []
                    for col_info in columns_info:
                        col_name, col_type = col_info[0], col_info[1]
                        
                        if col_name.lower() in ['end', 'start', 'order', 'group', 'select', 'from', 'where']:
                            safe_col_name = f'"{col_name}"'
                        else:
                            safe_col_name = col_name
                        column_defs.append(f"{safe_col_name} {col_type}")
                    
                    create_sql = f"CREATE OR REPLACE TABLE raw.{table_name} ({', '.join(column_defs)})"
                    self.conn.execute(create_sql)
                    
                                        
                    if data:
                        placeholders = ', '.join(['?' for _ in columns_info])
                        insert_sql = f"INSERT INTO raw.{table_name} VALUES ({placeholders})"
                        self.conn.executemany(insert_sql, data)
                    
                    logger.info(f"  ✓ raw.{table_name}: {len(data)} rows")
                    
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.error(f"  ✗ Failed to import table {table_name}: {e}")
        
        finally:
            source_conn.close()
        
        logger.info("✓ Raw data import complete")
    
    def _load_sql_file(self, file_path: Path) -> str:
        """Load the content of a SQL file"""
        try:
            with open(file_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                return f.read()
        except FileNotFoundError:
            logger.error(f"SQL file {file_path} not found")
            raise
    
    def _execute_sql(self, sql: str, table_name: str, layer_name: str) -> bool:
        """Execute SQL and create a table in the specified schema"""
        try:
            
            target_schema = self.schema_config[layer_name]
            full_table_name = f"{target_schema}.{table_name}"
            
                        
            create_table_sql = f"CREATE OR REPLACE TABLE {full_table_name} AS ({sql})"
            
            logger.info(f"Executing: {full_table_name}")
            self.conn.execute(create_table_sql)
            
                        
            count_result = self.conn.execute(f"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM {full_table_name}").fetchone()
            row_count = count_result[0] if count_result else 0
            
            logger.info(f"✓ {full_table_name}: {row_count:,} rows")
            return True
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"✗ {full_table_name}: {e}")
            return False
    
    def _execute_layer(self, layer_name: str) -> bool:
        """Execute all tables for a specified layer"""
        layer_config = self.config['layers'][layer_name]
        tables = layer_config.get('tables', [])
        
                
        execution_order = self.config.get('execution_order', {}).get(layer_name, tables)
        
        logger.info(f"\n=== Executing {layer_name.upper()} Layer ===")
        logger.info(f"Description: {layer_config.get('description', '')}")
        
        success_count = 0
        total_count = len(execution_order)
        
        for table_name in execution_order:
            
            sql_file_path = self.sql_dir / layer_name / f"{table_name}.sql"
            
            if not sql_file_path.exists():
                logger.warning(f"Skipping {table_name}: SQL file not found ({sql_file_path})")
                continue
            
            sql_content = self._load_sql_file(sql_file_path)
            if self._execute_sql(sql_content, table_name, layer_name):
                success_count += 1
        
        logger.info(f"{layer_name.upper()} layer complete: {success_count}/{total_count} tables created successfully")
        return success_count == total_count
    
    def _clean_generated_tables(self):
        """Clean all generated tables, preserving the raw data schema"""
        logger.info("🧼 Cleaning up previously generated schemas and tables...")
        
                
        schemas_to_clean = ['staging', 'intermediate', 'mart']
        
        for schema_name in schemas_to_clean:
            try:
                
                result = self.conn.execute(f"SELECT schema_name FROM information_schema.schemata WHERE schema_name = '{schema_name}'").fetchall()
                if result:
                    self.conn.execute(f"DROP SCHEMA IF EXISTS {schema_name} CASCADE")
                    logger.info(f"✓ Dropped schema: {schema_name}")
            except Exception as e:
                logger.warning(f"Failed to drop schema {schema_name}: {e}")
        
        logger.info("Cleanup complete")

    def run_pipeline(self, layers: Optional[List[str]] = None) -> bool:
        """Run the complete data pipeline"""
        if layers is None:
            layers = list(self.config['layers'].keys())
        
        logger.info("🚀 Starting Workday data pipeline execution")
        logger.info(f"Source database: {self.source_db_path}")
        logger.info(f"Target database: {self.target_db_path}")
        
                
        try:
            
            self._initialize_database()
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Failed to initialize database: {e}")
            
            if os.path.exists(self.target_db_path):
                logger.info("Attempting to continue with the existing database...")
                self.conn = duckdb.connect(self.target_db_path)
            else:
                raise

## agent 实际看到的执行结果

        
        self._clean_generated_tables()
        
                
        self._create_schemas()
        
        total_success = True
        
        for layer_name in layers:
            if layer_name not in self.config['layers']:
                logger.error(f"Unknown layer: {layer_name}")
                total_success = False
                continue
            
            dependencies = self.config['layers'][layer_name].get('dependencies', [])
            for dep in dependencies:
                if dep not in layers[:layers.index(layer_name)]:
                    logger.warning(f"{layer_name} depends on {dep}, but {dep} has not been executed yet")
            
            layer_success = self._execute_layer(layer_name)
            total_success = total_success and layer_success
        
        if total_success:
            logger.info("\n🎉 Workday data pipeline execution completed successfully!")
            logger.info(f"✨ The complete data warehouse has been generated: {self.target_db_path}")
            self._show_final_stats()
        else:
            logger.error("\n❌ Errors occurred during data pipeline execution")
        
        if self.conn:
            self.conn.close()
        
        return total_success
    
    def _show_final_stats(self):
        """Display final statistics - shown by Schema layer"""
        logger.info("\n=== Final Data Statistics (by Schema Layer) ===")
        
                
        for layer_name, schema_name in self.schema_config.items():
            try:
                
                tables_result = self.conn.execute(f"""SELECT table_name 
                                                     FROM information_schema.tables 
                                                     WHERE table_schema = '{schema_name}'""").fetchall()
                schema_tables = [table[0] for table in tables_result]
                
                if schema_tables:
                    logger.info(f"\n{layer_name.upper()} Layer (Schema: {schema_name}):")
                    for table in sorted(schema_tables):
                        try:
                            count = self.conn.execute(f"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM {schema_name}.{table}").fetchone()[0]
                            logger.info(f"  ✓ {schema_name}.{table}: {count:,} rows")
                        except Exception as e:
                            logger.info(f"  ✗ {schema_name}.{table}: query failed - {e}")
                else:
                    logger.info(f"\n{layer_name.upper()} Layer (Schema: {schema_name}): No tables")
                    
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"Failed to query schema {schema_name}: {e}")


def main():
    """Main function"""
    pipeline = WorkdayDataPipeline()
    
    success = pipeline.run_pipeline()
    if success:
        print("\n✅ Workday data transformation completed successfully!")
        print("📋 Transformation Results:")
        print(f"  📥 Source Database: {pipeline.source_db_path} (raw tables only)")
        print(f"  📤 Target Database: {pipeline.target_db_path} (full layered architecture)")
        print("\n🏗️ Data Architecture:")
        print("  📊 Staging Layer - Standardized raw Workday data")
        print("  🔄 Intermediate Layer - Business logic for employees and positions")
        print("  🎯 Mart Layer - Analysis-ready data for employees, positions, and organizations")
    else:
        print("\n❌ Errors occurred during data transformation, please check the logs.")

# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# PHASE 1: Defines the transformation from raw data to clean staging tables.  #
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
staging_contract:
  purpose: "Transform raw Workday data into clean, validated staging tables with standardized formats, proper data types, and comprehensive data quality checks. This phase ensures data integrity before applying business logic."
  
  quality_actions:
    correct:
      description: "Automatically fix deterministic format issues such as email casing, whitespace trimming, and standardized date formats"
      examples: ["LOWER(TRIM(email))", "standardize timezone formats", "normalize phone numbers"]
    
    delete_row:
      description: "Remove entire row when records contain severe data quality issues that make them unusable downstream."
      examples: ["Explicitly marked for deletion", "Confirmed duplicates", "Invalid primary keys", "Essential business attributes contain irrecoverable errors"]
    
    nullify_field:
      description: "Set invalid field values to NULL when the field is non-critical and record can still be useful"
      examples: ["malformed optional URLs", "invalid secondary contact information", "corrupted metadata fields"]

  tables:
    - name: stg_workday__worker
      description: "Standardized worker records containing core employee identifiers and basic employment status information"

### event 21

## 命令原文

mkdir -p sql/{staging,intermediate,marts}; cat > /tmp/gen.py <<'PY'
import yaml,os
x=yaml.safe_load(open('docs/data_contract.yaml')); tables=x['staging_contract']['tables']
# raw alias mapping
maps={'worker_id':'worker_id','source_relation':None,'personal_info_history_id':'personal_info_history_id','person_name_type':'name_type','email_id':'email_id','military_status':'status','organization_role_id':'role_id','organization_role_code':'org_role_code','organization_worker_code':'worker_id'}
# source rows with person_id require worker_to_person join
person_tables={'person_info_history','person_name','person_contact_email_address','personal_information_ethnicity','military_service'}
for t in tables:
 name=t['name']; raw=t['source_table'].split('.')[1]
 expressions=[]
 for col in t['columns']:
  n=col['name']; source=maps.get(n,n)
  if n=='source_relation': expr="'workday'::VARCHAR"
  elif n=='worker_id' and raw in person_tables: expr='w.worker_id::VARCHAR'
  elif raw in person_tables and n not in ('source_relation',): expr=f'r."{source}"'
  else: expr=f'r."{source}"'
  if n=='effective_start' and raw=='worker_history': pass
  typ=col['data_type']
  if n=='worker_id' and raw=='person_info_history': expr='w.worker_id::VARCHAR'
  elif n=='worker_id' and raw=='person_name': expr='w.worker_id::VARCHAR'
  elif n=='worker_id' and raw=='person_contact_email_address': expr='w.worker_id::VARCHAR'
  elif n=='worker_id' and raw=='personal_information_ethnicity': expr='w.worker_id::VARCHAR'
  elif n=='worker_id' and raw=='military_service': expr='w.worker_id::VARCHAR'
  if n=='organization_name': expr='TRIM(r.organization_name)'
  elif n=='employee_type': expr="CASE WHEN r.employee_type IS NOT NULL AND TRIM(r.employee_type) NOT IN ('Regular','Contingent','Intern','Contractor') THEN NULL ELSE TRIM(r.employee_type) END"
  elif n=='email_address': expr="CASE WHEN r.email_address IS NOT NULL AND r.email_address NOT LIKE '%@%' THEN NULL ELSE LOWER(TRIM(r.email_address)) END"
  elif n=='is_hispanic_or_latino': expr='COALESCE(r.is_hispanic_or_latino, FALSE)'
  elif typ in ('DATE','BIGINT','BOOLEAN'):
   expr=f' try_cast({expr} AS {typ})'
  else: expr=f'CAST({expr} AS {typ})'
  expressions.append(f'    {expr} AS {n}')
 where=[]
 for c in t['columns']:
  n=c['name']
  if n=='effective_start' and raw=='worker_history': where+= ["r.effective_start <= CURRENT_DATE", "r.effective_start >= DATE '1950-01-01'"]
  if n=='effective_end' and raw=='worker_history': where += ['(r.effective_end IS NULL OR r.effective_end >= r.effective_start)','(r.effective_end IS NULL OR r.effective_end <= CURRENT_DATE + INTERVAL \'30 days\')']
  if n=='date_of_birth': where += ["r.date_of_birth IS NULL OR (r.date_of_birth >= DATE '1930-01-01' AND r.date_of_birth <= CURRENT_DATE - INTERVAL '16 years')"]
  if n=='organization_name': where += ["r.organization_name IS NOT NULL", "LENGTH(TRIM(r.organization_name)) > 0"]
 # mandatory non-null values / deterministic invalid keys
 for c in t['columns']:
  if 'not_null' in c.get('constraints',[]):
   n=c['name']; source=maps.get(n,n)
   if n=='source_relation': continue
   if n=='worker_id' and raw in person_tables: where.append('w.worker_id IS NOT NULL')
   else: where.append(f'r."{source}" IS NOT NULL')
 joins=''
 if raw in person_tables:
  joins='\nJOIN raw.worker_to_person AS w ON r.person_id = w.person_id'
 sql='SELECT\n'+',\n'.join(expressions)+f'\nFROM raw.{raw} AS r{joins}'
 if where: sql+='\nWHERE '+'\n  AND '.join(f'({z})' for z in where)
 open(f'sql/staging/{name}.sql','w').write(sql)
PY
python3 /tmp/gen.py
