### event 23

## 命令原文

cat > sql/intermediate/int_workday__worker_details.sql <<'SQL'
SELECT
    worker_id,
    source_relation,
    worker_history_id,
    event_type,
    effective_start AS hire_date,
    effective_end AS departure_date,
    CASE WHEN effective_start IS NULL THEN NULL
         ELSE date_diff('day', effective_start, COALESCE(effective_end, CURRENT_DATE))::BIGINT END AS days_as_worker,
    (effective_end IS NULL) AS is_employed
FROM staging.stg_workday__worker
SQL
cat > sql/intermediate/int_workday__personal_details.sql <<'SQL'
WITH personal_base AS (
    SELECT worker_id, source_relation, personal_info_history_id, date_of_birth,
           marital_status, political_affiliation, city_of_birth, country_of_birth,
           last_medical_exam_date
    FROM staging.stg_workday__personal_information
), preferred_names AS (
    SELECT worker_id, source_relation, first_name, last_name, full_name
    FROM staging.stg_workday__person_name
    WHERE person_name_type IS NULL OR LOWER(person_name_type) = 'preferred'
), work_emails AS (
    SELECT worker_id, source_relation, email_address
    FROM staging.stg_workday__person_contact_email_address
    WHERE email_code IS NULL OR LOWER(email_code) LIKE '%work%'
), ethnicities AS (
    SELECT worker_id, source_relation,
           string_agg(DISTINCT ethnicity, ', ') AS ethnicity_codes
    FROM staging.stg_workday__personal_information_ethnicity
    WHERE ethnicity IS NOT NULL
    GROUP BY worker_id, source_relation
), military AS (
    SELECT worker_id, source_relation, TRUE AS is_military_service, military_status
    FROM staging.stg_workday__military_service
)
SELECT p.worker_id, p.source_relation, p.personal_info_history_id, p.date_of_birth,
       p.marital_status, p.political_affiliation, p.city_of_birth, p.country_of_birth,
       p.last_medical_exam_date, n.first_name, n.last_name, n.full_name,
       e.email_address, eth.ethnicity_codes, COALESCE(m.is_military_service, FALSE) AS is_military_service,
       m.military_status
FROM personal_base AS p
LEFT JOIN preferred_names AS n USING (worker_id, source_relation)
LEFT JOIN work_emails AS e USING (worker_id, source_relation)
LEFT JOIN ethnicities AS eth USING (worker_id, source_relation)
LEFT JOIN military AS m USING (worker_id, source_relation)
SQL
cat > sql/intermediate/int_workday__worker_position_enriched.sql <<'SQL'
SELECT wp.worker_id, wp.position_id, wp.source_relation,
       wp.effective_start AS position_start_date, wp.effective_end AS position_end_date,
       p.business_title, p.employee_type, p.default_weekly_hours,
       CASE WHEN wp.effective_start IS NULL THEN NULL
            ELSE date_diff('day', wp.effective_start, COALESCE(wp.effective_end, CURRENT_DATE))::BIGINT END AS days_in_position
FROM staging.stg_workday__worker_position AS wp
LEFT JOIN staging.stg_workday__position AS p ON wp.position_id = p.position_id

cat > sql/intermediate/int_workday__worker_employee_enhanced.sql <<'SQL'
WITH joined AS (
    SELECT d.worker_id, d.source_relation, d.worker_history_id, d.event_type, d.hire_date,
           d.departure_date, d.days_as_worker, d.is_employed,
           p.first_name, p.last_name, p.full_name, p.date_of_birth, p.marital_status,
           p.city_of_birth, p.country_of_birth, p.email_address, p.ethnicity_codes,
           p.is_military_service, p.military_status,
           pos.position_id, pos.business_title, pos.employee_type, pos.default_weekly_hours,
           pos.position_start_date, pos.position_end_date, pos.days_in_position
    FROM intermediate.int_workday__worker_details AS d
    LEFT JOIN intermediate.int_workday__personal_details AS p
      ON CAST(d.worker_id AS VARCHAR) = p.worker_id AND d.source_relation = p.source_relation
    LEFT JOIN intermediate.int_workday__worker_position_enriched AS pos
      ON CAST(d.worker_id AS VARCHAR) = CAST(pos.worker_id AS VARCHAR)
     AND d.source_relation = pos.source_relation
)
SELECT *,
       COALESCE(days_as_worker >= 365, FALSE) AS is_employed_one_year,
       COALESCE(days_as_worker >= 1825, FALSE) AS is_employed_five_years,
       COALESCE(days_as_worker >= 3650, FALSE) AS is_employed_ten_years,
       COALESCE(days_as_worker >= 365 AND is_employed, FALSE) AS is_current_employee_one_year,
       COALESCE(days_as_worker >= 1825 AND is_employed, FALSE) AS is_current_employee_five_years,
       COALESCE(days_as_worker >= 3650 AND is_employed, FALSE) AS is_current_employee_ten_years
FROM joined
SQL
cat > sql/intermediate/int_workday__employee_lifecycle.sql <<'SQL'
WITH employee_base AS (
    SELECT worker_id, source_relation, hire_date, departure_date, is_employed,
           days_as_worker, COALESCE(departure_date, CURRENT_DATE)::DATE AS analysis_end_date
    FROM intermediate.int_workday__worker_details
)
SELECT worker_id, source_relation, hire_date, departure_date, is_employed, days_as_worker,
       analysis_end_date,
       CASE WHEN days_as_worker >= 30 OR is_employed THEN 1 ELSE 0 END::INTEGER AS retained_30_days,
       CASE WHEN days_as_worker >= 90 OR is_employed THEN 1 ELSE 0 END::INTEGER AS retained_90_days,
       CASE WHEN days_as_worker >= 365 OR is_employed THEN 1 ELSE 0 END::INTEGER AS retained_1_year,
       CASE WHEN days_as_worker >= 1095 OR is_employed THEN 1 ELSE 0 END::INTEGER AS retained_3_years,
       CASE WHEN is_employed = FALSE THEN 'Churned' WHEN days_as_worker < 90 THEN 'High Risk'
            WHEN days_as_worker < 365 THEN 'Medium Risk' WHEN days_as_worker < 730 THEN 'Low Risk' ELSE 'Stable' END AS churn_risk_category,
       CASE WHEN days_as_worker < 90 THEN 'New Hire (0-3 months)' WHEN days_as_worker < 365 THEN 'Junior (3-12 months)'
            WHEN days_as_worker < 730 THEN 'Experienced (1-2 years)' WHEN days_as_worker < 1825 THEN 'Senior (2-5 years)' ELSE 'Veteran (5+ years)' END AS employee_maturity_segment,
       CASE WHEN is_employed = FALSE THEN CASE WHEN days_as_worker < 90 THEN 'Early Termination'
            WHEN days_as_worker < 365 THEN 'Short-term Departure' WHEN days_as_worker < 730 THEN 'Mid-term Departure' ELSE 'Long-term Departure' END
            ELSE 'Active Employee' END AS departure_category,
       CASE WHEN days_as_worker > 0 THEN ROUND(days_as_worker / 365.0, 2) ELSE 0 END::DOUBLE AS tenure_years,
       CASE WHEN hire_date IS NULL THEN NULL ELSE strftime(hire_date, '%Y') || '-Q' || CAST(((month(hire_date)-1)//3)+1 AS VARCHAR) END AS hire_quarter_cohort,
       year(hire_date)::BIGINT AS hire_year_cohort,
       CASE WHEN hire_date IS NULL OR (is_employed IS NULL AND departure_date IS NULL) THEN NULL
            WHEN is_employed THEN year(CURRENT_DATE)-year(hire_date) ELSE year(departure_date)-year(hire_date) END::BIGINT AS calendar_years_worked,
       CASE WHEN is_employed = FALSE THEN 0 WHEN days_as_worker < 30 THEN 10 WHEN days_as_worker < 90 THEN 25
            WHEN days_as_worker < 365 THEN 50 WHEN days_as_worker < 730 THEN 75 WHEN days_as_worker < 1825 THEN 90 ELSE 100 END::INTEGER AS retention_stability_score,
       month(hire_date)::BIGINT AS hire_month,
       CASE WHEN hire_date IS NULL THEN NULL ELSE (dayofweek(hire_date))::BIGINT END AS hire_day_of_week,
       CASE WHEN hire_date IS NULL THEN NULL WHEN month(hire_date) IN (12,1,2) THEN 'Winter'
            WHEN month(hire_date) IN (3,4,5) THEN 'Spring' WHEN month(hire_date) IN (6,7,8) THEN 'Summer' ELSE 'Fall' END AS hire_season,
       CASE WHEN hire_date > CURRENT_DATE THEN 'ERROR: Future hire date'
            WHEN departure_date < hire_date THEN 'ERROR: Departure before hire'
            WHEN days_as_worker < 0 THEN 'ERROR: Negative tenure' ELSE 'VALID' END AS data_quality_flag
FROM employee_base
SQL

### event 25

## 命令原文

cat > sql/intermediate/int_workday__organization_metrics.sql <<'SQL'
WITH active_org AS (
    SELECT * FROM staging.stg_workday__organization WHERE is_inactive = FALSE
), assignments AS (
    SELECT wpo.worker_id, wpo.position_id, wpo.organization_id, wpo.source_relation,
           wpo.effective_start, wpo.effective_end,
           p.employee_type, p.default_weekly_hours, jp.management_level, jp.compensation_grade_id,
           jp.is_work_shift_required, jp.is_union_eligible, jf.job_family_id, jfg.job_family_group_id
    FROM staging.stg_workday__worker_position_organization AS wpo
    LEFT JOIN staging.stg_workday__position AS p ON wpo.position_id=p.position_id
    LEFT JOIN staging.stg_workday__position_job_profile AS pjp ON p.position_id=pjp.position_id AND p.source_relation=pjp.source_relation
    LEFT JOIN staging.stg_workday__job_profile AS jp ON pjp.job_profile_id=jp.job_profile_id AND pjp.source_relation=jp.source_relation
    LEFT JOIN staging.stg_workday__job_family_job_profile AS jfjp ON jp.job_profile_id=jfjp.job_profile_id AND jp.source_relation=jfjp.source_relation
    LEFT JOIN staging.stg_workday__job_family AS jf ON jfjp.job_family_id=jf.job_family_id AND jfjp.source_relation=jf.source_relation
    LEFT JOIN staging.stg_workday__job_family_job_family_group AS jfjg ON jf.job_family_id=jfjg.job_family_id AND jf.source_relation=jfjg.source_relation
    LEFT JOIN staging.stg_workday__job_family_group AS jfg ON jfjg.job_family_group_id=jfg.job_family_group_id AND jfjg.source_relation=jfg.source_relation
), current_stats AS (
 SELECT organization_id, source_relation, count(DISTINCT worker_id) current_employee_count,
 count(DISTINCT position_id) total_positions,
 count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE employee_type='Regular') regular_employees,
 count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE employee_type='Contingent') contingent_employees,
 count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE employee_type='Intern') intern_employees,
 coalesce(avg(coalesce(default_weekly_hours,40)),40)::DOUBLE avg_weekly_hours,
 coalesce(sum(coalesce(default_weekly_hours,40)),0)::HUGEINT total_weekly_hours,
 count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE management_level='Manager') managers_count,
 count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE management_level='Senior Manager') senior_managers_count,
 count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE management_level='Director') directors_count,
 count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE management_level='Individual Contributor') individual_contributors,
 count(DISTINCT compensation_grade_id) unique_compensation_grades,
 count(DISTINCT job_family_id) unique_job_families,count(DISTINCT job_family_group_id) unique_job_family_groups,
 count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE is_work_shift_required) shift_required_positions,
 count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE is_union_eligible) union_eligible_positions,
 avg(date_diff('day',effective_start,coalesce(effective_end,current_date))) avg_tenure_days
 FROM assignments WHERE effective_end IS NULL GROUP BY organization_id,source_relation
), mobility AS (
 SELECT a.organization_id,a.source_relation,
 count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE effective_start >= current_date - INTERVAL '1 year') new_hires_last_year,
 count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE effective_end >= current_date - INTERVAL '1 year') departures_last_year,
 count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE effective_start >= current_date - INTERVAL '1 year' AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM assignments x WHERE x.worker_id=a.worker_id AND x.source_relation=a.source_relation AND x.organization_id<>a.organization_id AND x.effective_start<a.effective_start)) internal_transfers_in,
 count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE effective_end >= current_date - INTERVAL '1 year' AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM assignments x WHERE x.worker_id=a.worker_id AND x.source_relation=a.source_relation AND x.organization_id<>a.organization_id AND x.effective_start>a.effective_end)) internal_transfers_out
 FROM assignments a GROUP BY organization_id,source_relation
), grade_freq AS (
 SELECT organization_id,source_relation,compensation_grade_id,
 row_number() over(partition by organization_id,source_relation order by count(*) desc, compensation_grade_id asc) rn
 FROM assignments WHERE effective_end IS NULL AND compensation_grade_id IS NOT NULL
 GROUP BY organization_id,source_relation,compensation_grade_id
)
SELECT o.organization_id,o.source_relation,o.organization_name,o.organization_code,o.organization_type,o.organization_sub_type,o.staffing_model,
 coalesce(c.current_employee_count,0)::BIGINT current_employee_count,coalesce(c.total_positions,0)::BIGINT total_positions,
 coalesce(c.regular_employees,0)::BIGINT regular_employees,coalesce(c.contingent_employees,0)::BIGINT contingent_employees,coalesce(c.intern_employees,0)::BIGINT intern_employees,
 coalesce(c.avg_weekly_hours,40)::DOUBLE avg_weekly_hours,coalesce(c.total_weekly_hours,0)::HUGEINT total_weekly_hours,
 coalesce(c.managers_count,0)::BIGINT managers_count,coalesce(c.senior_managers_count,0)::BIGINT senior_managers_count,coalesce(c.directors_count,0)::BIGINT directors_count,coalesce(c.individual_contributors,0)::BIGINT individual_contributors,
 coalesce(m.new_hires_last_year,0)::BIGINT new_hires_last_year,coalesce(m.departures_last_year,0)::BIGINT departures_last_year,
 coalesce(m.internal_transfers_in,0)::BIGINT internal_transfers_in,coalesce(m.internal_transfers_out,0)::BIGINT internal_transfers_out,
 coalesce(c.avg_tenure_days,0)::DOUBLE avg_tenure_days,coalesce(c.unique_compensation_grades,0)::BIGINT unique_compensation_grades,
 coalesce(g.compensation_grade_id,'Unknown')::VARCHAR most_common_grade,coalesce(c.unique_job_families,0)::BIGINT unique_job_families,coalesce(c.unique_job_family_groups,0)::BIGINT unique_job_family_groups,
 coalesce(c.shift_required_positions,0)::BIGINT shift_required_positions,coalesce(c.union_eligible_positions,0)::BIGINT union_eligible_positions,
 CASE WHEN coalesce(c.current_employee_count,0)=0 THEN 0 ELSE
 (20 + CASE WHEN coalesce(m.departures_last_year,0)*1.0/c.current_employee_count<0.2 THEN 20 ELSE 0 END+
 CASE WHEN c.managers_count*1.0/c.current_employee_count BETWEEN .1 AND .3 THEN 20 ELSE 0 END+
 CASE WHEN c.unique_job_families>=2 THEN 20 ELSE 0 END+CASE WHEN c.avg_tenure_days>=365 THEN 20 ELSE 0 END)::INTEGER END organization_health_score,
 CASE WHEN coalesce(c.total_positions,0)>0 THEN round(c.current_employee_count*100.0/c.total_positions,2) ELSE 0 END::DOUBLE position_fill_rate,
 CASE WHEN coalesce(c.current_employee_count,0)>0 THEN round(coalesce(m.departures_last_year,0)*100.0/c.current_employee_count,2) ELSE 0 END::DOUBLE annual_turnover_rate,
 CASE WHEN coalesce(c.current_employee_count,0)>0 THEN round(coalesce(c.managers_count,0)*100.0/c.current_employee_count,2) ELSE 0 END::DOUBLE management_ratio,
 CASE WHEN coalesce(c.current_employee_count,0)=0 THEN 'Needs Attention' WHEN c.current_employee_count>0 AND (20+CASE WHEN coalesce(m.departures_last_year,0)*1.0/c.current_employee_count<.2 THEN 20 ELSE 0 END+CASE WHEN c.managers_count*1.0/c.current_employee_count BETWEEN .1 AND .3 THEN 20 ELSE 0 END+CASE WHEN c.unique_job_families>=2 THEN 20 ELSE 0 END+CASE WHEN c.avg_tenure_days>=365 THEN 20 ELSE 0)>=80 THEN 'High Performance'
 WHEN coalesce(c.current_employee_count,0)>0 THEN CASE WHEN (20+CASE WHEN coalesce(m.departures_last_year,0)*1.0/c.current_employee_count<.2 THEN 20 ELSE 0 END+CASE WHEN c.managers_count*1.0/c.current_employee_count BETWEEN .1 AND .3 THEN 20 ELSE 0 END+CASE WHEN c.unique_job_families>=2 THEN 20 ELSE 0 END+CASE WHEN c.avg_tenure_days>=365 THEN 20 ELSE 0)>=60 THEN 'Good Performance' WHEN (20+CASE WHEN coalesce(m.departures_last_year,0)*1.0/c.current_employee_count<.2 THEN 20 ELSE 0 END+CASE WHEN c.managers_count*1.0/c.current_employee_count BETWEEN .1 AND .3 THEN 20 ELSE 0 END+CASE WHEN c.unique_job_families>=2 THEN 20 ELSE 0 END+CASE WHEN c.avg_tenure_days>=365 THEN 20 ELSE 0)>=40 THEN 'Average Performance' WHEN (20+CASE WHEN coalesce(m.departures_last_year,0)*1.0/c.current_employee_count<.2 THEN 20 ELSE 0 END+CASE WHEN c.managers_count*1.0/c.current_employee_count BETWEEN .1 AND .3 THEN 20 ELSE 0 END+CASE WHEN c.unique_job_families>=2 THEN 20 ELSE 0 END+CASE WHEN c.avg_tenure_days>=365 THEN 20 ELSE 0)>=20 THEN 'Below Average' ELSE 'Needs Attention' END END performance_category,
 CASE WHEN coalesce(c.current_employee_count,0)=0 THEN 'Empty' WHEN c.current_employee_count<=10 THEN 'Small Team' WHEN c.current_employee_count<=50 THEN 'Medium Team' WHEN c.current_employee_count<=200 THEN 'Large Team' ELSE 'Enterprise Unit' END organization_size_category,
 CASE WHEN c.current_employee_count<0 THEN 'ERROR' WHEN c.current_employee_count>c.total_positions OR (c.current_employee_count>0 AND coalesce(m.departures_last_year,0)*100.0/c.current_employee_count>100) OR (c.current_employee_count>0 AND coalesce(c.managers_count,0)*100.0/c.current_employee_count>50) THEN 'WARNING' ELSE 'VALID' END data_quality_flag
FROM active_org o LEFT JOIN current_stats c USING(organization_id,source_relation)
LEFT JOIN mobility m USING(organization_id,source_relation)
LEFT JOIN grade_freq g ON o.organization_id=g.organization_id AND o.source_relation=g.source_relation AND g.rn=1
SQL

### event 27

## 命令原文

cat > sql/intermediate/int_workday__career_progression.sql <<'SQL'
WITH timeline AS (
 SELECT wp.worker_id,wp.source_relation,wp.position_id,wp.effective_start,wp.effective_end,
 row_number() over(partition by worker_id,source_relation order by effective_start) position_sequence,
 date_diff('day',effective_start,coalesce(effective_end,current_date))::BIGINT days_in_position,
 lead(position_id) over(partition by worker_id,source_relation order by effective_start) next_position_id,
 lead(effective_start) over(partition by worker_id,source_relation order by effective_start) next_position_start_date
 FROM staging.stg_workday__worker_position wp
), enriched AS (
 SELECT t.*,p.business_title,p.employee_type,p.default_weekly_hours,jp.job_profile_title,jp.management_level,jp.compensation_grade_id,jp.is_work_shift_required,jp.is_union_eligible,
 np.business_title next_business_title,njp.management_level next_management_level,njp.compensation_grade_id next_compensation_grade_id,
 np.default_weekly_hours next_weekly_hours
 FROM timeline t LEFT JOIN staging.stg_workday__position p ON t.position_id=p.position_id AND t.source_relation=p.source_relation
 LEFT JOIN staging.stg_workday__position_job_profile pjp ON p.position_id=pjp.position_id AND p.source_relation=pjp.source_relation
 LEFT JOIN staging.stg_workday__job_profile jp ON pjp.job_profile_id=jp.job_profile_id AND pjp.source_relation=jp.source_relation
 LEFT JOIN staging.stg_workday__position np ON t.next_position_id=np.position_id
 LEFT JOIN staging.stg_workday__position_job_profile npjp ON np.position_id=npjp.position_id AND np.source_relation=npjp.source_relation
 LEFT JOIN staging.stg_workday__job_profile njp ON npjp.job_profile_id=njp.job_profile_id AND npjp.source_relation=njp.source_relation
), moves AS (
 SELECT *, CASE WHEN next_position_id IS NULL THEN 'Current/Final Position'
 WHEN management_level IS DISTINCT FROM next_management_level THEN
 CASE WHEN management_level='Individual Contributor' AND next_management_level='Manager' THEN 'Promoted to Management'
 WHEN management_level='Manager' AND next_management_level='Senior Manager' THEN 'Management Promotion'
 WHEN management_level='Senior Manager' AND next_management_level='Director' THEN 'Executive Promotion'
 WHEN management_level<>'Individual Contributor' AND next_management_level='Individual Contributor' THEN 'Demotion' ELSE 'Level Change' END
 WHEN compensation_grade_id IS DISTINCT FROM next_compensation_grade_id THEN 'Grade Change'
 WHEN business_title IS DISTINCT FROM next_business_title THEN 'Lateral Move' ELSE 'Position Transfer' END career_move_type,
 CASE WHEN days_in_position<90 THEN 'Very Short (<3 months)' WHEN days_in_position<365 THEN 'Short (3-12 months)' WHEN days_in_position<730 THEN 'Medium (1-2 years)' WHEN days_in_position<1825 THEN 'Long (2-5 years)' ELSE 'Very Long (5+ years)' END position_tenure_category,
 (next_position_start_date IS NOT NULL AND date_diff('day',effective_start,next_position_start_date)<=365 AND management_level IS DISTINCT FROM next_management_level) is_fast_promotion,
 CASE WHEN position_sequence=1 THEN 'Entry Position' WHEN next_position_id IS NULL THEN 'Current Position' ELSE 'Career Progression' END career_stage,
 CASE WHEN next_position_id IS NULL THEN 'N/A' WHEN coalesce(default_weekly_hours,40)<coalesce(next_weekly_hours,40) THEN 'Increased Workload' WHEN coalesce(default_weekly_hours,40)>coalesce(next_weekly_hours,40) THEN 'Decreased Workload' ELSE 'Same Workload' END workload_change
 FROM enriched
), summaries AS (
 SELECT worker_id,source_relation,count(*)::BIGINT total_positions_held,min(effective_start) career_start_date,
 max(coalesce(next_position_start_date,effective_start)) latest_position_date,sum(days_in_position)::HUGEINT total_career_days,avg(days_in_position)::DOUBLE avg_position_tenure_days,
 count(*) FILTER(WHERE career_move_type LIKE '%Promotion%')::BIGINT total_promotions,
 count(*) FILTER(WHERE career_move_type='Demotion')::BIGINT total_demotions,count(*) FILTER(WHERE career_move_type='Lateral Move')::BIGINT lateral_moves,
 count(*) FILTER(WHERE is_fast_promotion)::BIGINT fast_promotions,
 count(*) FILTER(WHERE management_level IS DISTINCT FROM 'Individual Contributor')::BIGINT management_positions_held,
 max(CASE management_level WHEN 'Director' THEN 4 WHEN 'Senior Manager' THEN 3 WHEN 'Manager' THEN 2 WHEN 'Individual Contributor' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::INTEGER highest_management_level_reached,
 count(DISTINCT compensation_grade_id)::BIGINT unique_compensation_grades,
 count(*) FILTER(WHERE is_work_shift_required)::BIGINT shift_work_positions,count(*) FILTER(WHERE is_union_eligible)::BIGINT union_eligible_positions
 FROM moves GROUP BY worker_id,source_relation
), scored AS (
 SELECT m.*,s.* EXCLUDE(worker_id,source_relation),
 CASE WHEN s.avg_position_tenure_days>=730 THEN 'High Stability' WHEN s.avg_position_tenure_days>=365 THEN 'Medium Stability' WHEN s.avg_position_tenure_days>=180 THEN 'Low Stability' ELSE 'Very Low Stability' END career_stability_category,
 CASE WHEN s.total_promotions>=3 THEN 'Fast Career Growth' WHEN s.total_promotions>=1 THEN 'Moderate Career Growth' ELSE 'Stable Career' END career_growth_pace,
 least(100,CASE WHEN s.total_promotions>=2 THEN 30 ELSE s.total_promotions*15 END+CASE WHEN s.highest_management_level_reached>=3 THEN 25 ELSE s.highest_management_level_reached*8 END+CASE WHEN s.avg_position_tenure_days>=365 THEN 20 ELSE 0 END+CASE WHEN s.unique_compensation_grades>=3 THEN 15 ELSE s.unique_compensation_grades*5 END+CASE WHEN s.total_career_days>=1095 THEN 10 ELSE 0 END)::BIGINT career_development_score,
 CASE WHEN m.position_sequence=1 AND m.next_position_id IS NULL THEN 'Single Position Career' WHEN m.position_sequence<=2 THEN 'Early Career' WHEN m.position_sequence<=5 THEN 'Mid Career' ELSE 'Senior Career' END career_phase
 FROM moves m JOIN summaries s USING(worker_id,source_relation)
)
SELECT worker_id,source_relation,position_id,effective_start,effective_end,position_sequence,days_in_position,business_title,employee_type,default_weekly_hours,job_profile_title,management_level,compensation_grade_id,is_work_shift_required,is_union_eligible,career_move_type,position_tenure_category,is_fast_promotion,career_stage,workload_change,
 total_positions_held,career_start_date,latest_position_date,total_career_days,avg_position_tenure_days,total_promotions,total_demotions,lateral_moves,fast_promotions,management_positions_held,highest_management_level_reached,unique_compensation_grades,shift_work_positions,union_eligible_positions,career_stability_category,career_growth_pace,career_development_score,career_phase,
 CASE WHEN days_in_position<0 THEN 'ERROR: Negative tenure' WHEN effective_start>CURRENT_DATE THEN 'ERROR: Future start date' WHEN effective_end<effective_start THEN 'ERROR: End date before start date' WHEN total_promotions>total_positions_held THEN 'ERROR: Promotions exceed positions' ELSE 'VALID' END data_quality_flag
FROM scored
SQL

### event 31

## 命令原文

cat > sql/marts/workday__employee_overview.sql <<'SQL'
WITH base AS (
 SELECT e.*, md5(coalesce(cast(worker_id as varchar),'_dbt_utils_surrogate_key_null_')||'-'||coalesce(source_relation,'_dbt_utils_surrogate_key_null_')||'-'||coalesce(position_id,'_dbt_utils_surrogate_key_null_')||'-'||coalesce(cast(position_start_date as varchar),'_dbt_utils_surrogate_key_null_')) employee_id
 FROM intermediate.int_workday__worker_employee_enhanced e
), career AS (
 SELECT worker_id,source_relation,position_id,max(total_positions_held) total_positions_held,max(total_promotions) total_promotions,
 max(total_demotions) total_demotions,max(lateral_moves) lateral_moves,max(fast_promotions) fast_promotions,max(management_positions_held) management_positions_held,
 max(highest_management_level_reached) highest_management_level_reached,
 mode() within group(order by career_stability_category) career_stability_category,
 mode() within group(order by career_growth_pace) career_growth_pace,avg(career_development_score) career_development_score,
 mode() within group(order by career_phase) career_phase
 FROM intermediate.int_workday__career_progression GROUP BY worker_id,source_relation,position_id
), org AS (
 SELECT w.worker_id,w.source_relation,o.organization_id,o.organization_name,o.organization_type,o.organization_sub_type,
 o.current_employee_count,o.organization_health_score,o.annual_turnover_rate,o.management_ratio,o.performance_category,o.organization_size_category
 FROM staging.stg_workday__worker_position_organization w
 LEFT JOIN intermediate.int_workday__organization_metrics o ON w.organization_id=o.organization_id AND w.source_relation=o.source_relation
 WHERE w.effective_end IS NULL
), comp AS (
 SELECT p.position_id,p.source_relation,jp.compensation_grade_id,jp.is_work_shift_required,jp.is_union_eligible
 FROM staging.stg_workday__position_job_profile p
 LEFT JOIN staging.stg_workday__job_profile jp ON p.job_profile_id=jp.job_profile_id AND p.source_relation=jp.source_relation
), enriched AS (
 SELECT b.*,l.retained_30_days,l.retained_90_days,l.retained_1_year,l.retained_3_years,l.churn_risk_category,l.employee_maturity_segment,l.departure_category,l.tenure_years,
 l.hire_quarter_cohort,l.hire_year_cohort,l.retention_stability_score,l.hire_month,l.hire_season,
 c.total_positions_held,c.total_promotions,c.total_demotions,c.lateral_moves,c.fast_promotions,c.management_positions_held,c.highest_management_level_reached,c.career_stability_category,c.career_growth_pace,c.career_development_score,c.career_phase,
 o.organization_id,o.organization_name,o.organization_type,o.organization_sub_type,o.current_employee_count,o.organization_health_score,o.annual_turnover_rate,o.management_ratio,o.performance_category,o.organization_size_category,
 comp.compensation_grade_id,comp.is_work_shift_required,comp.is_union_eligible
 FROM base b LEFT JOIN intermediate.int_workday__employee_lifecycle l USING(worker_id,source_relation)
 LEFT JOIN career c ON b.worker_id=c.worker_id AND b.source_relation=c.source_relation AND b.position_id=c.position_id
 LEFT JOIN org o ON b.worker_id=o.worker_id AND b.source_relation=o.source_relation
 LEFT JOIN comp ON b.position_id=comp.position_id AND b.source_relation=comp.source_relation
), calc AS (
 SELECT *, CASE WHEN date_of_birth IS NULL THEN NULL ELSE date_diff('year',date_of_birth,current_date)-CASE WHEN strftime(current_date,'%m-%d')<strftime(date_of_birth,'%m-%d') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END END::BIGINT age,
 CASE WHEN days_as_worker<365 THEN '< 1 year' WHEN days_as_worker<730 THEN '1-2 years' WHEN days_as_worker<1825 THEN '2-5 years' WHEN days_as_worker<3650 THEN '5-10 years' ELSE '10+ years' END tenure_group,
 CASE WHEN regexp_extract(coalesce(compensation_grade_id,''),'([0-9]+)',1)='' THEN 'Ungraded' WHEN try_cast(regexp_extract(compensation_grade_id,'([0-9]+)',1) AS INTEGER)<=5 THEN 'Entry Level' WHEN try_cast(regexp_extract(compensation_grade_id,'([0-9]+)',1) AS INTEGER)<=10 THEN 'Mid Level' WHEN try_cast(regexp_extract(compensation_grade_id,'([0-9]+)',1) AS INTEGER)<=15 THEN 'Senior Level' ELSE 'Executive Level' END compensation_tier,
 (CASE WHEN coalesce(is_work_shift_required,FALSE) THEN 0 ELSE 1 END + CASE WHEN coalesce(is_union_eligible,FALSE) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END + CASE WHEN default_weekly_hours<=40 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::INTEGER work_conditions_score
 FROM enriched
), finalized AS (
 SELECT *,round(coalesce(retention_stability_score,0)*.3+coalesce(career_development_score,0)*.4+coalesce(organization_health_score,0)*.2+work_conditions_score*10*.1,2)::DOUBLE overall_employee_score,
 CASE WHEN is_employed=FALSE THEN 'Former Employee' WHEN churn_risk_category='High Risk' THEN 'High Risk' WHEN churn_risk_category='Medium Risk' AND coalesce(career_development_score,0)<40 THEN 'Medium-High Risk' WHEN churn_risk_category='Medium Risk' THEN 'Medium Risk' WHEN churn_risk_category='Low Risk' AND coalesce(annual_turnover_rate,0)>20 THEN 'Medium-Low Risk' ELSE 'Low Risk' END employee_risk_level,
 CASE WHEN is_employed=FALSE THEN 'Former Employee' WHEN career_development_score>=80 AND retention_stability_score>=80 THEN 'High Value - High Retention' WHEN career_development_score>=80 THEN 'High Value - Flight Risk' WHEN retention_stability_score>=80 THEN 'Stable - Development Needed' WHEN career_development_score>=60 THEN 'Moderate Value' ELSE 'Development Required' END employee_value_segment
 FROM calc
)
SELECT employee_id,worker_id,source_relation,position_id,position_start_date,worker_history_id,event_type,is_employed,hire_date,departure_date,days_as_worker,first_name,last_name,full_name,date_of_birth,marital_status,city_of_birth,country_of_birth,email_address,ethnicity_codes,is_military_service,military_status,business_title,employee_type,default_weekly_hours,position_end_date,days_in_position,
 is_employed_one_year,is_employed_five_years,is_employed_ten_years,is_current_employee_one_year,is_current_employee_five_years,is_current_employee_ten_years,age,tenure_group,
 coalesce(retained_30_days,0)::INTEGER retained_30_days,coalesce(retained_90_days,0)::INTEGER retained_90_days,coalesce(retained_1_year,0)::INTEGER retained_1_year,coalesce(retained_3_years,0)::INTEGER retained_3_years,
 coalesce(churn_risk_category,'Unknown') churn_risk_category,coalesce(employee_maturity_segment,'Unknown') employee_maturity_segment,coalesce(departure_category,'Active Employee') departure_category,coalesce(tenure_years,0)::DOUBLE tenure_years,
 coalesce(hire_quarter_cohort,'Unknown') hire_quarter_cohort,coalesce(hire_year_cohort,year(hire_date))::BIGINT hire_year_cohort,coalesce(retention_stability_score,0)::INTEGER retention_stability_score,coalesce(hire_month,month(hire_date))::BIGINT hire_month,coalesce(hire_season,'Unknown') hire_season,
 coalesce(total_positions_held,1)::BIGINT total_positions_held,coalesce(total_promotions,0)::BIGINT total_promotions,coalesce(total_demotions,0)::BIGINT total_demotions,coalesce(lateral_moves,0)::BIGINT lateral_moves,coalesce(fast_promotions,0)::BIGINT fast_promotions,coalesce(management_positions_held,0)::BIGINT management_positions_held,coalesce(highest_management_level_reached,1)::INTEGER highest_management_level_reached,
 coalesce(career_stability_category,'Unknown') career_stability_category,coalesce(career_growth_pace,'Stable Career') career_growth_pace,coalesce(career_development_score,0)::DOUBLE career_development_score,coalesce(career_phase,'Unknown') career_phase,
 coalesce(organization_id,'Unknown') organization_id,coalesce(organization_name,'Unknown') organization_name,coalesce(organization_type,'Unknown') organization_type,coalesce(organization_sub_type,'Unknown') organization_sub_type,coalesce(current_employee_count,0)::BIGINT dept_size,coalesce(organization_health_score,0)::INTEGER dept_health_score,coalesce(annual_turnover_rate,0)::DOUBLE dept_turnover_rate,coalesce(management_ratio,0)::DOUBLE dept_management_ratio,coalesce(performance_category,'Unknown') dept_performance_category,coalesce(organization_size_category,'Unknown') dept_size_category,
 coalesce(compensation_grade_id,'Ungraded') compensation_grade_id,compensation_tier,coalesce(is_work_shift_required,FALSE) is_work_shift_required,coalesce(is_union_eligible,FALSE) is_union_eligible,work_conditions_score,overall_employee_score,employee_risk_level,employee_value_segment,
 CASE WHEN age<16 OR age>80 THEN 'ERROR: Invalid age' WHEN hire_date>CURRENT_DATE THEN 'ERROR: Future hire date' WHEN departure_date<hire_date THEN 'ERROR: Departure before hire' WHEN days_as_worker<0 THEN 'ERROR: Negative tenure' WHEN overall_employee_score<0 OR overall_employee_score>100 THEN 'ERROR: Score out of range' ELSE 'VALID' END comprehensive_data_quality_flag,
 current_timestamp::TIMESTAMPTZ analysis_timestamp
FROM finalized

WITH base AS (SELECT * FROM mart.workday__employee_overview), metrics AS (
 SELECT 'workday'::VARCHAR source_relation,current_date::DATE analysis_date,
 count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE is_employed) active_employees,count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE NOT is_employed) former_employees,count(DISTINCT worker_id) total_employees_all_time,count(DISTINCT position_id) total_positions,
 count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE is_employed AND employee_type='Regular') regular_employees,count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE is_employed AND employee_type='Contingent') contingent_employees,count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE is_employed AND employee_type='Intern') intern_employees,
 avg(default_weekly_hours) FILTER(WHERE is_employed) avg_weekly_hours,sum(default_weekly_hours) FILTER(WHERE is_employed)::HUGEINT total_weekly_hours,
 count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE is_employed AND default_weekly_hours<40) part_time_employees,count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE is_employed AND default_weekly_hours>=40) full_time_employees,
 count(DISTINCT ethnicity_codes) FILTER(WHERE is_employed AND ethnicity_codes IS NOT NULL) ethnic_diversity_count,count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE is_employed AND is_military_service) military_veterans,avg(age) FILTER(WHERE is_employed) avg_employee_age,
 count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE is_employed AND age BETWEEN 18 AND 25) gen_z_employees,count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE is_employed AND age BETWEEN 26 AND 41) millennial_employees,count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE is_employed AND age BETWEEN 42 AND 57) gen_x_employees,count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE is_employed AND age>=58) boomer_employees,
 round(avg(retained_30_days) FILTER(WHERE is_employed)*100,2) retention_rate_30_days,round(avg(retained_90_days) FILTER(WHERE is_employed)*100,2) retention_rate_90_days,round(avg(retained_1_year) FILTER(WHERE is_employed)*100,2) retention_rate_1_year,round(avg(retained_3_years) FILTER(WHERE is_employed)*100,2) retention_rate_3_years,
 round(count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE NOT is_employed AND departure_date>=current_date-INTERVAL '1 year')*100.0/NULLIF(count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE hire_date<=current_date-INTERVAL '1 year'),0),2) annual_turnover_rate,
 round(avg(days_as_worker) FILTER(WHERE is_employed)/365.0,2) avg_tenure_years,round(avg(days_as_worker) FILTER(WHERE NOT is_employed)/365.0,2) avg_tenure_departed_years,
 count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE churn_risk_category='High Risk') high_risk_employees,count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE churn_risk_category='Medium Risk') medium_risk_employees,count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE churn_risk_category='Low Risk') low_risk_employees,count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE churn_risk_category='Stable') stable_employees,
 count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE departure_category='Early Termination') early_terminations,count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE departure_category='Short-term Departure') short_term_departures,count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE departure_category='Mid-term Departure') mid_term_departures,count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE departure_category='Long-term Departure') long_term_departures,
 round(avg(career_development_score) FILTER(WHERE is_employed),2) avg_career_development_score,count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE is_employed AND total_promotions>=2) high_achievers,count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE is_employed AND fast_promotions>0) fast_track_employees,
 count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE is_employed AND highest_management_level_reached>=4) directors_and_above,count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE is_employed AND highest_management_level_reached=3) senior_managers,count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE is_employed AND highest_management_level_reached=2) managers,count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE is_employed AND highest_management_level_reached=1) individual_contributors,
 count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE is_employed AND career_phase='Early Career') early_career_employees,count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE is_employed AND career_phase='Mid Career') mid_career_employees,count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE is_employed AND career_phase='Senior Career') senior_career_employees,
 count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE is_employed AND employee_value_segment='High Value - High Retention') high_value_retained,count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE is_employed AND employee_value_segment='High Value - Flight Risk') high_value_flight_risk,count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE is_employed AND employee_value_segment='Stable - Development Needed') stable_development_needed,count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE is_employed AND employee_value_segment='Development Required') development_required
 FROM base
), calc AS (SELECT *, round(active_employees*100.0/NULLIF(total_positions,0),2) position_utilization_rate,
 round(regular_employees*100.0/NULLIF(active_employees,0),2) regular_employee_percentage,round(full_time_employees*100.0/NULLIF(active_employees,0),2) full_time_percentage,round(military_veterans*100.0/NULLIF(active_employees,0),2) veteran_percentage,round(high_risk_employees*100.0/NULLIF(active_employees,0),2) high_risk_percentage,round(high_achievers*100.0/NULLIF(active_employees,0),2) high_achiever_percentage,round((directors_and_above+senior_managers+managers)*100.0/NULLIF(active_employees,0),2) management_percentage,
 round(25+CASE WHEN retention_rate_1_year>=80 THEN 25 ELSE retention_rate_1_year*.3125 END+CASE WHEN annual_turnover_rate<=15 THEN 25 ELSE greatest(0,25-(annual_turnover_rate-15)*1.67) END+CASE WHEN avg_career_development_score>=70 THEN 25 ELSE avg_career_development_score*.357 END,2) workforce_health_score FROM metrics)
SELECT *,CASE WHEN active_employees=0 THEN 'ERROR: No active employees' WHEN annual_turnover_rate>50 THEN 'WARNING: High turnover rate' WHEN avg_career_development_score<30 THEN 'WARNING: Low career development' ELSE 'HEALTHY' END workforce_status_flag,current_timestamp::TIMESTAMPTZ analysis_timestamp FROM calc

cat > sql/marts/workday__organization_performance.sql <<'SQL'
WITH org AS (SELECT * FROM intermediate.int_workday__organization_metrics), emp AS (
 SELECT organization_id,source_relation,
 count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE employee_value_segment='High Value - High Retention') high_value_retained_count,
 count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE employee_value_segment='High Value - Flight Risk') high_value_flight_risk_count,
 count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE employee_value_segment='Stable - Development Needed') stable_development_count,
 count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE employee_value_segment='Development Required') development_required_count,
 avg(career_development_score) FILTER(WHERE is_employed) avg_career_development_score,
 count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE is_employed AND total_promotions>=2) high_achievers_count,count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE is_employed AND fast_promotions>0) fast_track_count,
 count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE churn_risk_category='High Risk') high_risk_employees,count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE churn_risk_category='Medium Risk') medium_risk_employees,count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE churn_risk_category IN ('Low Risk','Stable')) low_risk_employees,
 avg(overall_employee_score) FILTER(WHERE is_employed) avg_employee_satisfaction_proxy,count(DISTINCT ethnicity_codes) FILTER(WHERE is_employed AND ethnicity_codes IS NOT NULL) ethnic_diversity_count,count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE is_employed AND is_military_service) military_veterans_count,
 avg(age) FILTER(WHERE is_employed) avg_employee_age,count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE is_employed AND age BETWEEN 18 AND 30) young_professionals_count,count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE is_employed AND age BETWEEN 31 AND 45) mid_career_professionals_count,count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE is_employed AND age BETWEEN 46 AND 60) senior_professionals_count,count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE is_employed AND age>60) pre_retirement_professionals_count
 FROM mart.workday__employee_overview WHERE organization_id<>'Unknown' GROUP BY organization_id,source_relation
), joined AS (SELECT o.*,coalesce(e.high_value_retained_count,0) high_value_retained_count,coalesce(e.high_value_flight_risk_count,0) high_value_flight_risk_count,coalesce(e.stable_development_count,0) stable_development_count,coalesce(e.development_required_count,0) development_required_count,coalesce(e.avg_career_development_score,0) avg_career_development_score,coalesce(e.high_achievers_count,0) high_achievers_count,coalesce(e.fast_track_count,0) fast_track_count,coalesce(e.high_risk_employees,0) high_risk_employees,coalesce(e.medium_risk_employees,0) medium_risk_employees,coalesce(e.low_risk_employees,0) low_risk_employees,coalesce(e.avg_employee_satisfaction_proxy,0) avg_employee_satisfaction_proxy,coalesce(e.ethnic_diversity_count,0) ethnic_diversity_count,coalesce(e.military_veterans_count,0) military_veterans_count,coalesce(e.avg_employee_age,0) avg_employee_age,coalesce(e.young_professionals_count,0) young_professionals_count,coalesce(e.mid_career_professionals_count,0) mid_career_professionals_count,coalesce(e.senior_professionals_count,0) senior_professionals_count,coalesce(e.pre_retirement_professionals_count,0) pre_retirement_professionals_count FROM org o LEFT JOIN emp e USING(organization_id,source_relation))
SELECT organization_id,source_relation,organization_name,organization_code,organization_type,organization_sub_type,staffing_model,current_employee_count,total_positions,position_fill_rate,organization_size_category,regular_employees,contingent_employees,intern_employees,
 round(regular_employees*100.0/NULLIF(current_employee_count,0),2)::DOUBLE regular_employee_percentage,avg_weekly_hours,total_weekly_hours,round(total_weekly_hours*1.0/NULLIF(current_employee_count,0),2)::DOUBLE avg_weekly_hours_per_employee,
managers_count,senior_managers_count,directors_count,individual_contributors,management_ratio,CASE WHEN individual_contributors=0 THEN 0 WHEN managers_count+senior_managers_count+directors_count=0 THEN NULL ELSE round(individual_contributors*1.0/(managers_count+senior_managers_count+directors_count),2)::FLOAT END span_of_control,
new_hires_last_year,departures_last_year,internal_transfers_in,internal_transfers_out,annual_turnover_rate,avg_tenure_days,round(avg_tenure_days/365.0,2)::DOUBLE avg_tenure_years,
CASE WHEN avg_tenure_days>=1095 THEN 'Mature Organization' WHEN avg_tenure_days>=730 THEN 'Developing Organization' WHEN avg_tenure_days>=365 THEN 'Growing Organization' ELSE 'New Organization' END organization_maturity,
unique_compensation_grades,most_common_grade,unique_job_families,unique_job_family_groups,round(unique_compensation_grades*100.0/NULLIF(current_employee_count,0),2)::FLOAT talent_density_ratio,
high_value_retained_count,high_value_flight_risk_count,stable_development_count,development_required_count,
round(high_value_retained_count*100.0/NULLIF(current_employee_count,0),2) high_value_retention_rate,round(high_value_flight_risk_count*100.0/NULLIF(current_employee_count,0),2) talent_flight_risk_rate,
avg_career_development_score,high_achievers_count,fast_track_count,round(high_achievers_count*100.0/NULLIF(current_employee_count,0),2) high_achiever_percentage,high_risk_employees,medium_risk_employees,low_risk_employees,
round(high_risk_employees*100.0/NULLIF(current_employee_count,0),2) high_risk_percentage,avg_employee_satisfaction_proxy,ethnic_diversity_count,military_veterans_count,round(avg_employee_age,1) avg_employee_age,young_professionals_count,mid_career_professionals_count,senior_professionals_count,pre_retirement_professionals_count,organization_health_score,performance_category,
round(least(30,avg_career_development_score*.3)+CASE WHEN annual_turnover_rate<=10 THEN 25 WHEN annual_turnover_rate<=20 THEN 20 WHEN annual_turnover_rate<=30 THEN 15 ELSE 10 END+least(20,avg_employee_satisfaction_proxy*.2)+CASE WHEN span_of_control BETWEEN 5 AND 12 THEN 15 WHEN span_of_control BETWEEN 3 AND 15 THEN 10 ELSE 5 END+10,2)::DOUBLE enhanced_organization_effectiveness_score,
CASE WHEN annual_turnover_rate>40 THEN 'Critical Risk' WHEN annual_turnover_rate>25 OR high_risk_employees>current_employee_count*.3 THEN 'High Risk' WHEN annual_turnover_rate>15 OR high_risk_employees>current_employee_count*.2 THEN 'Medium Risk' WHEN annual_turnover_rate<=10 AND avg_career_development_score>=70 THEN 'Low Risk' ELSE 'Moderate Risk' END organization_risk_level,
CASE WHEN annual_turnover_rate>25 THEN 'Focus on Retention Strategy' WHEN avg_career_development_score<50 THEN 'Enhance Career Development Programs' WHEN management_ratio>30 THEN 'Optimize Management Structure' WHEN avg_employee_satisfaction_proxy<60 THEN 'Improve Employee Experience' ELSE 'Maintain Current Excellence' END recommended_action,
CASE WHEN current_employee_count=0 THEN 'ERROR: No employees in organization' WHEN annual_turnover_rate>100 THEN 'ERROR: Turnover rate exceeds 100%' WHEN management_ratio>80 THEN 'WARNING: Extremely high management ratio' WHEN avg_career_development_score=0 THEN 'WARNING: No career development data' ELSE 'VALID' END data_quality_flag,current_timestamp::TIMESTAMPTZ analysis_timestamp
FROM joined WHERE current_employee_count>0

### event 37

## 命令原文

cat > sql/marts/workday__organization_overview.sql <<'SQL'
WITH active_org AS (SELECT * FROM staging.stg_workday__organization WHERE is_inactive=FALSE), rel AS (
 SELECT worker_id,organization_id,source_relation,position_id,effective_start,effective_end,
 date_diff('day',effective_start,coalesce(effective_end,current_date)) duration,
 CASE WHEN effective_end IS NULL THEN 'Active Assignment' ELSE 'Former Assignment' END status
 FROM staging.stg_workday__worker_position_organization
), stats AS (
 SELECT organization_id,source_relation,count(DISTINCT worker_id) total_employees_ever,count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE effective_end IS NULL) current_active_employees,count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE effective_end IS NOT NULL) former_employees,count(DISTINCT position_id) total_positions,count(DISTINCT position_id) FILTER(WHERE effective_end IS NULL) active_positions_count,
 count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE effective_start>=current_date-INTERVAL '1 year') new_assignments_last_year,count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE effective_end>=current_date-INTERVAL '1 year') departures_last_year,avg(duration) FILTER(WHERE effective_end IS NULL) avg_current_days,avg(duration) FILTER(WHERE effective_end IS NOT NULL) avg_former_days,max(effective_start) last_activity_date,max(effective_end) last_departure_date
 FROM rel GROUP BY organization_id,source_relation
), roles AS (
 SELECT ow.organization_id,'workday' source_relation,count(DISTINCT r.role_id) unique_organization_roles,count(DISTINCT ow.worker_id) FILTER(WHERE r.org_role_code ILIKE '%Manager%') management_role_employees,count(DISTINCT ow.worker_id) FILTER(WHERE r.org_role_code ILIKE '%Director%') director_role_employees,count(DISTINCT ow.worker_id) FILTER(WHERE r.org_role_code ILIKE '%Lead%') lead_role_employees FROM staging.stg_workday__organization_role_worker ow LEFT JOIN staging.stg_workday__organization_role r ON ow.organization_id=r.organization_id AND ow.role_id=r.organization_role_id GROUP BY ow.organization_id
), perf AS (
 SELECT organization_id,source_relation,avg(overall_employee_score) FILTER(WHERE is_employed) avg_employee_performance_score,avg(career_development_score) FILTER(WHERE is_employed) avg_career_development_score,avg(retention_stability_score) FILTER(WHERE is_employed) avg_retention_stability_score,
 count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE employee_value_segment='High Value - High Retention') high_value_stable_employees,count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE employee_value_segment='High Value - Flight Risk') high_value_flight_risk_employees,count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE employee_value_segment='Stable - Development Needed') stable_development_employees,count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE employee_value_segment='Development Required') development_required_employees,
 count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE employee_risk_level='High Risk') high_risk_employees,count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE employee_risk_level='Medium Risk') medium_risk_employees,count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE employee_risk_level='Low Risk') low_risk_employees,
 count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE is_employed AND employee_maturity_segment LIKE '%New Hire%') new_hire_employees,count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE is_employed AND employee_maturity_segment LIKE '%Junior%') junior_employees,count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE is_employed AND employee_maturity_segment LIKE '%Experienced%') experienced_employees,count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE is_employed AND employee_maturity_segment LIKE '%Senior%') senior_employees,count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE is_employed AND employee_maturity_segment LIKE '%Veteran%') veteran_employees,
 count(DISTINCT ethnicity_codes) FILTER(WHERE is_employed AND ethnicity_codes IS NOT NULL) ethnic_diversity_count,count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE is_employed AND is_military_service) military_veterans_count,avg(age) FILTER(WHERE is_employed) avg_employee_age,
 count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE is_employed AND compensation_tier='Entry Level') entry_level_employees,count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE is_employed AND compensation_tier='Mid Level') mid_level_employees,count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE is_employed AND compensation_tier='Senior Level') senior_level_employees,count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE is_employed AND compensation_tier='Executive Level') executive_level_employees,
 sum(total_promotions) FILTER(WHERE is_employed) total_promotions_in_organization,count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE is_employed AND total_promotions>=2) high_achievers_count,count(DISTINCT worker_id) FILTER(WHERE is_employed AND fast_promotions>0) fast_track_employees_count
 FROM mart.workday__employee_overview WHERE organization_id<>'Unknown' GROUP BY organization_id,source_relation
), joined AS (SELECT o.*,s.*,r.unique_organization_roles,r.management_role_employees,r.director_role_employees,r.lead_role_employees,p.* EXCLUDE(organization_id,source_relation) FROM active_org o LEFT JOIN stats s USING(organization_id,source_relation) LEFT JOIN roles r USING(organization_id,source_relation) LEFT JOIN perf p USING(organization_id,source_relation))
SELECT organization_id,source_relation,organization_name,organization_code,organization_type,organization_sub_type,staffing_model,visibility,
CASE visibility WHEN 'Public' THEN 'Open Access' WHEN 'Private' THEN 'Restricted Access' ELSE 'Unknown Access' END access_level,effective_start organization_created_date,date_diff('day',effective_start,current_date)::BIGINT organization_age_days,round(date_diff('day',effective_start,current_date)/365.0,2)::DOUBLE organization_age_years,
CASE WHEN date_diff('day',effective_start,current_date)<365 THEN 'New' WHEN date_diff('day',effective_start,current_date)<1095 THEN 'Young' WHEN date_diff('day',effective_start,current_date)<3650 THEN 'Mature' ELSE 'Legacy' END organization_maturity_level,
CASE organization_type WHEN 'Company' THEN 'Corporate Level' WHEN 'Division' THEN 'Division Level' WHEN 'Department' THEN 'Department Level' WHEN 'Team' THEN 'Team Level' ELSE 'Other Level' END organizational_level,
coalesce(total_employees_ever,0)::BIGINT total_employees_ever,coalesce(current_active_employees,0)::BIGINT current_active_employees,coalesce(former_employees,0)::BIGINT former_employees,coalesce(total_positions,0)::BIGINT total_positions,coalesce(active_positions_count,0)::BIGINT active_positions_count,
round(coalesce(current_active_employees,0)*100.0/NULLIF(total_positions,0),2)::DOUBLE position_fill_rate,coalesce(new_assignments_last_year,0)::BIGINT new_assignments_last_year,coalesce(departures_last_year,0)::BIGINT departures_last_year,round(coalesce(departures_last_year,0)*100.0/NULLIF(current_active_employees,0),2)::DOUBLE annual_turnover_rate,
round(coalesce(avg_current_days,0)/365.0,2)::DOUBLE avg_current_tenure_years,round(coalesce(avg_former_days,0)/365.0,2)::DOUBLE avg_former_tenure_years,coalesce(unique_organization_roles,0)::BIGINT unique_organization_roles,coalesce(management_role_employees,0)::BIGINT management_role_employees,coalesce(director_role_employees,0)::BIGINT director_role_employees,coalesce(lead_role_employees,0)::BIGINT lead_role_employees,
round((coalesce(management_role_employees,0)+coalesce(director_role_employees,0))*100.0/NULLIF(current_active_employees,0),2)::DOUBLE management_ratio,coalesce(avg_employee_performance_score,0)::DOUBLE avg_employee_performance_score,coalesce(avg_career_development_score,0)::DOUBLE avg_career_development_score,coalesce(avg_retention_stability_score,0)::DOUBLE avg_retention_stability_score,
coalesce(high_value_stable_employees,0)::BIGINT high_value_stable_employees,coalesce(high_value_flight_risk_employees,0)::BIGINT high_value_flight_risk_employees,coalesce(stable_development_employees,0)::BIGINT stable_development_employees,coalesce(development_required_employees,0)::BIGINT development_required_employees,coalesce(high_risk_employees,0)::BIGINT high_risk_employees,coalesce(medium_risk_employees,0)::BIGINT medium_risk_employees,coalesce(low_risk_employees,0)::BIGINT low_risk_employees,
round(coalesce(high_risk_employees,0)*100.0/NULLIF(current_active_employees,0),2)::DOUBLE high_risk_employee_percentage,coalesce(new_hire_employees,0)::BIGINT new_hire_employees,coalesce(junior_employees,0)::BIGINT junior_employees,coalesce(experienced_employees,0)::BIGINT experienced_employees,coalesce(senior_employees,0)::BIGINT senior_employees,coalesce(veteran_employees,0)::BIGINT veteran_employees,coalesce(ethnic_diversity_count,0)::BIGINT ethnic_diversity_count,coalesce(military_veterans_count,0)::BIGINT military_veterans_count,coalesce(avg_employee_age,0)::DOUBLE avg_employee_age,
coalesce(entry_level_employees,0)::BIGINT entry_level_employees,coalesce(mid_level_employees,0)::BIGINT mid_level_employees,coalesce(senior_level_employees,0)::BIGINT senior_level_employees,coalesce(executive_level_employees,0)::BIGINT executive_level_employees,coalesce(total_promotions_in_organization,0)::HUGEINT total_promotions_in_organization,coalesce(high_achievers_count,0)::BIGINT high_achievers_count,coalesce(fast_track_employees_count,0)::BIGINT fast_track_employees_count,
round(least(30,coalesce(avg_employee_performance_score,0)*.3)+CASE WHEN departures_last_year=0 THEN 25 WHEN annual_turnover_rate<=10 THEN 25 WHEN annual_turnover_rate<=20 THEN 20 WHEN annual_turnover_rate<=30 THEN 15 WHEN annual_turnover_rate<=40 THEN 10 ELSE 5 END+least(20,coalesce(avg_career_development_score,0)*.2)+CASE WHEN position_fill_rate>=90 THEN 15 WHEN position_fill_rate>=75 THEN 12 WHEN position_fill_rate>=60 THEN 9 ELSE 6 END+CASE WHEN high_risk_employee_percentage<=10 THEN 10 WHEN high_risk_employee_percentage<=20 THEN 7 WHEN high_risk_employee_percentage<=30 THEN 4 ELSE 2 END,2)::DOUBLE organization_health_score,
CASE WHEN avg_employee_performance_score>=80 THEN 'Excellent' WHEN avg_employee_performance_score>=60 THEN 'Good' WHEN avg_employee_performance_score>=40 THEN 'Average' ELSE 'Needs Improvement' END performance_category,
CASE WHEN current_active_employees=0 THEN 'Empty' WHEN current_active_employees<=5 THEN 'Micro' WHEN current_active_employees<=15 THEN 'Small' WHEN current_active_employees<=50 THEN 'Medium' WHEN current_active_employees<=200 THEN 'Large' ELSE 'Enterprise' END organization_size_category,
coalesce(last_activity_date,effective_start) last_activity_date,coalesce(last_departure_date,DATE '1900-01-01') last_departure_date,
CASE WHEN current_active_employees=0 THEN 'Review Organization Necessity' WHEN annual_turnover_rate>30 THEN 'Focus on Retention' WHEN high_risk_employee_percentage>25 THEN 'Address Employee Risk' WHEN position_fill_rate<70 THEN 'Improve Staffing' WHEN avg_employee_performance_score<50 THEN 'Enhance Performance Management' WHEN management_ratio>40 THEN 'Optimize Structure' ELSE 'Maintain Excellence' END management_recommendation,
CASE WHEN organization_name IS NULL OR trim(organization_name)='' THEN 'ERROR: Missing organization name' WHEN current_active_employees>total_positions+10 THEN 'WARNING: Employees exceed positions' WHEN annual_turnover_rate>100 THEN 'WARNING: Turnover exceeds 100%' WHEN organization_age_days<0 THEN 'ERROR: Negative organization age' ELSE 'VALID' END data_quality_flag,current_timestamp::TIMESTAMPTZ analysis_timestamp
FROM joined

cat > sql/marts/workday__job_overview.sql <<'SQL'
WITH family AS (
 SELECT jfjp.job_profile_id,jf.source_relation,count(DISTINCT jf.job_family_id) associated_job_families_count,count(DISTINCT jfg.job_family_group_id) associated_job_family_groups_count,
 string_agg(DISTINCT jf.job_family_code,', ') FILTER(WHERE jf.job_family_code IS NOT NULL) job_family_codes,string_agg(DISTINCT jf.job_family_name,', ') FILTER(WHERE jf.job_family_name IS NOT NULL) job_family_names,
 string_agg(DISTINCT jfg.job_family_group_code,', ') FILTER(WHERE jfg.job_family_group_code IS NOT NULL) job_family_group_codes,string_agg(DISTINCT jfg.job_family_group_name,', ') FILTER(WHERE jfg.job_family_group_name IS NOT NULL) job_family_group_names
 FROM staging.stg_workday__job_family_job_profile jfjp LEFT JOIN staging.stg_workday__job_family jf ON jfjp.job_family_id=jf.job_family_id AND jfjp.source_relation=jf.source_relation
 LEFT JOIN staging.stg_workday__job_family_job_family_group jfjg ON jf.job_family_id=jfjg.job_family_id AND jf.source_relation=jfjg.source_relation
 LEFT JOIN staging.stg_workday__job_family_group jfg ON jfjg.job_family_group_id=jfg.job_family_group_id AND jfjg.source_relation=jfg.source_relation
 GROUP BY jfjp.job_profile_id,jf.source_relation
), positions AS (
 SELECT pjp.job_profile_id,pjp.source_relation,count(DISTINCT p.position_id) total_positions_count,count(DISTINCT p.position_id) FILTER(WHERE wpo.effective_end IS NULL) active_positions_count,
 count(DISTINCT p.position_id) FILTER(WHERE wpo.effective_end IS NOT NULL) inactive_positions_count,count(DISTINCT wpo.worker_id) FILTER(WHERE wpo.effective_end IS NULL) current_employees_count,count(DISTINCT wpo.worker_id) total_employees_ever_count,
 avg(p.default_weekly_hours) avg_weekly_hours,min(p.default_weekly_hours) min_weekly_hours,max(p.default_weekly_hours) max_weekly_hours,
 count(DISTINCT wpo.worker_id) FILTER(WHERE wpo.effective_end IS NULL AND p.employee_type='Regular') regular_employees_count,count(DISTINCT wpo.worker_id) FILTER(WHERE wpo.effective_end IS NULL AND p.employee_type='Contingent') contingent_employees_count,count(DISTINCT wpo.worker_id) FILTER(WHERE wpo.effective_end IS NULL AND p.employee_type='Intern') intern_employees_count,
 count(DISTINCT wpo.organization_id) organizations_using_job_count
 FROM staging.stg_workday__position_job_profile pjp LEFT JOIN staging.stg_workday__position p ON pjp.position_id=p.position_id AND pjp.source_relation=p.source_relation
 LEFT JOIN staging.stg_workday__worker_position_organization wpo ON p.position_id=wpo.position_id AND p.source_relation=wpo.source_relation GROUP BY pjp.job_profile_id,pjp.source_relation
), ep AS (
 SELECT pjp.job_profile_id,pjp.source_relation,
 avg(e.career_development_score) FILTER(WHERE e.is_employed) avg_career_development_score,count(DISTINCT e.worker_id) FILTER(WHERE e.is_employed AND e.total_promotions>=2) high_achievers_count,count(DISTINCT e.worker_id) FILTER(WHERE e.is_employed AND e.fast_promotions>0) fast_track_employees_count,
 count(DISTINCT e.worker_id) FILTER(WHERE e.churn_risk_category='High Risk') high_risk_employees_count,count(DISTINCT e.worker_id) FILTER(WHERE e.churn_risk_category='Medium Risk') medium_risk_employees_count,count(DISTINCT e.worker_id) FILTER(WHERE e.churn_risk_category IN('Low Risk','Stable')) stable_employees_count,
 count(DISTINCT e.worker_id) FILTER(WHERE e.is_employed AND e.employee_maturity_segment LIKE '%New Hire%') new_hires_count,count(DISTINCT e.worker_id) FILTER(WHERE e.is_employed AND e.employee_maturity_segment LIKE '%Junior%') junior_employees_count,count(DISTINCT e.worker_id) FILTER(WHERE e.is_employed AND e.employee_maturity_segment LIKE '%Experienced%') experienced_employees_count,count(DISTINCT e.worker_id) FILTER(WHERE e.is_employed AND e.employee_maturity_segment LIKE '%Senior%') senior_employees_count,count(DISTINCT e.worker_id) FILTER(WHERE e.is_employed AND e.employee_maturity_segment LIKE '%Veteran%') veteran_employees_count,
 avg(e.age) FILTER(WHERE e.is_employed) avg_employee_age,count(DISTINCT e.worker_id) FILTER(WHERE e.is_employed AND e.age BETWEEN 18 AND 30) young_professionals_count,count(DISTINCT e.worker_id) FILTER(WHERE e.is_employed AND e.age BETWEEN 31 AND 45) mid_career_professionals_count,count(DISTINCT e.worker_id) FILTER(WHERE e.is_employed AND e.age BETWEEN 46 AND 60) senior_professionals_count,
 count(DISTINCT e.worker_id) FILTER(WHERE e.employee_value_segment='High Value - High Retention') high_value_retained_count,count(DISTINCT e.worker_id) FILTER(WHERE e.employee_value_segment='High Value - Flight Risk') high_value_flight_risk_count,
 count(DISTINCT e.worker_id) FILTER(WHERE e.is_employed AND e.compensation_tier='Entry Level') entry_level_employees_count,count(DISTINCT e.worker_id) FILTER(WHERE e.is_employed AND e.compensation_tier='Mid Level') mid_level_employees_count,count(DISTINCT e.worker_id) FILTER(WHERE e.is_employed AND e.compensation_tier='Senior Level') senior_level_employees_count,count(DISTINCT e.worker_id) FILTER(WHERE e.is_employed AND e.compensation_tier='Executive Level') executive_level_employees_count
 FROM staging.stg_workday__position_job_profile pjp LEFT JOIN mart.workday__employee_overview e ON pjp.position_id=e.position_id AND pjp.source_relation=e.source_relation GROUP BY pjp.job_profile_id,pjp.source_relation
)
SELECT jp.job_profile_id,jp.source_relation,jp.job_profile_code,jp.job_profile_title job_title,jp.job_profile_summary job_summary,jp.management_level,jp.compensation_grade_id,jp.is_work_shift_required,jp.is_union_eligible,
coalesce(f.job_family_codes,'Unknown') job_family_codes,coalesce(f.job_family_names,'Unknown') job_family_names,coalesce(f.job_family_group_codes,'Unknown') job_family_group_codes,coalesce(f.job_family_group_names,'Unknown') job_family_group_names,coalesce(f.associated_job_families_count,0)::BIGINT associated_job_families_count,coalesce(f.associated_job_family_groups_count,0)::BIGINT associated_job_family_groups_count,
coalesce(p.total_positions_count,0)::BIGINT total_positions_count,coalesce(p.active_positions_count,0)::BIGINT active_positions_count,coalesce(p.inactive_positions_count,0)::BIGINT inactive_positions_count,coalesce(p.current_employees_count,0)::BIGINT current_employees_count,coalesce(p.total_employees_ever_count,0)::BIGINT total_employees_ever_count,
coalesce(p.avg_weekly_hours,40)::DOUBLE avg_weekly_hours,coalesce(p.min_weekly_hours,0)::BIGINT min_weekly_hours,coalesce(p.max_weekly_hours,0)::BIGINT max_weekly_hours,coalesce(p.regular_employees_count,0)::BIGINT regular_employees_count,coalesce(p.contingent_employees_count,0)::BIGINT contingent_employees_count,coalesce(p.intern_employees_count,0)::BIGINT intern_employees_count,coalesce(p.organizations_using_job_count,0)::BIGINT organizations_using_job_count,'Unknown'::VARCHAR primary_organization_id,
coalesce(e.avg_career_development_score,0)::DOUBLE avg_career_development_score,coalesce(e.high_achievers_count,0)::BIGINT high_achievers_count,coalesce(e.fast_track_employees_count,0)::BIGINT fast_track_employees_count,coalesce(e.high_risk_employees_count,0)::BIGINT high_risk_employees_count,coalesce(e.medium_risk_employees_count,0)::BIGINT medium_risk_employees_count,coalesce(e.stable_employees_count,0)::BIGINT stable_employees_count,coalesce(e.new_hires_count,0)::BIGINT new_hires_count,coalesce(e.junior_employees_count,0)::BIGINT junior_employees_count,coalesce(e.experienced_employees_count,0)::BIGINT experienced_employees_count,coalesce(e.senior_employees_count,0)::BIGINT senior_employees_count,coalesce(e.veteran_employees_count,0)::BIGINT veteran_employees_count,
coalesce(round(e.avg_employee_age,1),0)::DOUBLE avg_employee_age,coalesce(e.young_professionals_count,0)::BIGINT young_professionals_count,coalesce(e.mid_career_professionals_count,0)::BIGINT mid_career_professionals_count,coalesce(e.senior_professionals_count,0)::BIGINT senior_professionals_count,coalesce(e.high_value_retained_count,0)::BIGINT high_value_retained_count,coalesce(e.high_value_flight_risk_count,0)::BIGINT high_value_flight_risk_count,coalesce(e.entry_level_employees_count,0)::BIGINT entry_level_employees_count,coalesce(e.mid_level_employees_count,0)::BIGINT mid_level_employees_count,coalesce(e.senior_level_employees_count,0)::BIGINT senior_level_employees_count,coalesce(e.executive_level_employees_count,0)::BIGINT executive_level_employees_count,
CASE WHEN coalesce(p.total_positions_count,0)=0 THEN 'No Positions' WHEN p.total_positions_count<=2 THEN 'Very Rare' WHEN p.total_positions_count<=5 THEN 'Rare' WHEN p.total_positions_count<=15 THEN 'Uncommon' WHEN p.total_positions_count<=50 THEN 'Common' ELSE 'Very Common' END job_rarity_level,
CASE WHEN coalesce(p.current_employees_count,0)=0 THEN 'No Current Employees' WHEN p.current_employees_count*1.0/NULLIF(p.total_positions_count,0)>=.9 THEN 'High' WHEN p.current_employees_count*1.0/NULLIF(p.total_positions_count,0)>=.7 THEN 'Moderate' WHEN p.current_employees_count*1.0/NULLIF(p.total_positions_count,0)>=.5 THEN 'Low' ELSE 'Very Low' END talent_competition_level,
CASE WHEN coalesce(e.avg_career_development_score,0)>=80 THEN 'Highly Developed Role' WHEN e.avg_career_development_score>=60 THEN 'Well Developed Role' WHEN e.avg_career_development_score>=40 THEN 'Moderately Developed Role' WHEN e.avg_career_development_score>=20 THEN 'Developing Role' ELSE 'New/Emerging Role' END job_maturity_level,
CASE WHEN coalesce(p.current_employees_count,0)=0 THEN 'No Data' WHEN e.high_risk_employees_count*1.0/p.current_employees_count>=.3 THEN 'High' WHEN e.high_risk_employees_count*1.0/p.current_employees_count>=.2 THEN 'Moderate' WHEN e.high_risk_employees_count*1.0/p.current_employees_count>=.1 THEN 'Low' ELSE 'Excellent' END retention_difficulty_level,
round(coalesce(e.high_achievers_count,0)*100.0/NULLIF(p.current_employees_count,0),2)::FLOAT high_performer_percentage,round(coalesce(e.fast_track_employees_count,0)*100.0/NULLIF(p.current_employees_count,0),2)::FLOAT career_growth_potential_percentage,
round((CASE WHEN coalesce(p.current_employees_count,0)>0 THEN 20 ELSE 0 END+least(25,coalesce(e.avg_career_development_score,0)*.357)+least(25,coalesce(e.high_achievers_count,0)*100.0/NULLIF(p.current_employees_count,0)*.833)+CASE WHEN coalesce(e.high_risk_employees_count,0)=0 THEN 15 WHEN e.high_risk_employees_count*1.0/NULLIF(p.current_employees_count,0)<=.1 THEN 10 ELSE 5 END+least(15,coalesce(p.organizations_using_job_count,0)*5)),2)::DOUBLE job_health_score,
CASE WHEN coalesce(p.current_employees_count,0)=0 THEN 'Inactive Job' WHEN e.high_achievers_count*100.0/NULLIF(p.current_employees_count,0)>=40 AND e.avg_career_development_score>=80 THEN 'Strategic High-Value Role' WHEN e.high_achievers_count*100.0/NULLIF(p.current_employees_count,0)>=25 AND e.avg_career_development_score>=60 THEN 'High-Value Role' WHEN e.high_achievers_count*100.0/NULLIF(p.current_employees_count,0)>=15 AND e.avg_career_development_score>=40 THEN 'Standard Value Role' WHEN e.high_achievers_count*100.0/NULLIF(p.current_employees_count,0)>=10 THEN 'Developing Role' ELSE 'Entry-Level Role' END job_value_tier,
CASE WHEN coalesce(p.current_employees_count,0)=0 THEN 'Review Job Necessity' WHEN e.high_risk_employees_count*1.0/NULLIF(p.current_employees_count,0)>.3 THEN 'Focus on Retention' WHEN coalesce(e.high_achievers_count,0)*100.0/NULLIF(p.current_employees_count,0)<15 THEN 'Enhance Performance Management' WHEN coalesce(e.avg_career_development_score,0)<50 THEN 'Improve Career Development' WHEN coalesce(p.organizations_using_job_count,0)=1 THEN 'Consider Cross-Organizational Expansion' ELSE 'Maintain Excellence' END talent_management_recommendation,
CASE WHEN jp.job_profile_title IS NULL OR trim(jp.job_profile_title)='' THEN 'ERROR: Missing job title' WHEN p.total_positions_count<0 THEN 'ERROR: Negative position count' WHEN p.current_employees_count>p.total_positions_count THEN 'WARNING: More employees than positions' WHEN p.avg_weekly_hours>80 THEN 'WARNING: Excessive working hours' ELSE 'VALID' END data_quality_flag,current_timestamp::TIMESTAMPTZ analysis_timestamp
FROM staging.stg_workday__job_profile jp LEFT JOIN family f USING(job_profile_id,source_relation) LEFT JOIN positions p USING(job_profile_id,source_relation) LEFT JOIN ep e USING(job_profile_id,source_relation)
